- **Startup**, with that build: 381 dependency points match (the 14 new ones keep the snapshot's fields, the wars and the war); 102 hooks, 4 writes.
- **Main menu**: `country get CAS` answered `not_in_game: the main menu is open`, exit code 1.
- **Castile's numbers**: `treasury` 213.793 against 213 in the top bar; `manpower` 0 against 0; `max_manpower` 30065 against the manpower tooltip's limit of 30,065 men; `stability` 0.0 against +0; `prestige` 80.398 against +80 (and +0.80 legitimacy from prestige in the legitimacy tooltip); `power` 565, 278, 481 against 565, 278 and 481 in the top bar and the court page. After running the game to 1470.5.15: 218.299 against 218, prestige 80.242 against +80, points 581, 288, 491 against the same, `max_manpower` 30068.
- **Castile's relations**: `ally` POL, HAB, PAP against the diplomacy page's alliances "波兰, 奥地利与教宗国"; `subject` BUR, GUY, TOU, ARA, NAP, TLC against its personal unions "勃艮第, 阿拉贡与那不勒斯" and vassals "加斯科涅, 图卢兹与特莱姆森", which the page shows on two rows; `rival` TUR against the rivals row, the Ottomans and two empty slots. The page's enemies row (the Ottomans and France) are countries that rival Castile, which a country's own snapshot does not list; the Ottomans' snapshot has `rival` CAS.
- **The war**: `name` "卡斯蒂利亚对奥斯曼的十字军", the war panel's title; `attackers` CAS, BUR, GUY, TOU, ARA, TLC, NAP, the seven the war panel lists, Castile first in both (the panel orders the rest otherwise); `defenders` TUR, DUL, the panel's two with the Ottomans starred as leader, and "交战中：奥斯曼*与杜勒卡迪尔" on the diplomacy page.
- **Another country**: the Ottomans read `stability` 1.0, `prestige` 46.788, `manpower` 3555, the same war, `ally` DUL and `rival` HAB, MAM, CAS; their diplomacy panel showed stability +1, prestige 46, a manpower reserve of 3k, the war with Castile, an alliance with Dulkadir, rivals Austria, the Mamluks and Castile, and no subjects. Gascony and Aragon read `overlord` CAS; Poland `ally` BRA and CAS, `subject` MOL and LIT.
- **Errors**: `QQQ` `not_found: no country QQQ`; `C1` exit code 2 (not a tag), nothing sent. `ZZZ` is Zazzau and read as a country; `REB`, the rebels, read with no relations or wars.
- **Costs**: 0.9 to 1.3 ms each in the engine; the core log has no error, and no extension was stopped.
- **Not seen in the running game**: the extension side, `GameApi::country` and the SDK's `Game::country`, since no extension calls it (unit tests, since the change to JSON below: the core's writer and the SDK's reader against `abi/json/country.json`, the SDK through a table built as the core builds its own, a newer core's members and relation kinds, and a core whose table ends before it); a relation or a war changing between two reads (no alliance made or broken, no rival chosen, no war declared or ended), which C8 will do; among subjects, marches, tributaries and colonial nations, since only personal unions and vassals were seen; another country's treasury and monarch points, which the game shows only for the player's country; a war name in another language; the observer's `not_in_game`.
- **Cleanup**: loading the save rewrote `Castilla-5.eu4` and its backup, and `settings.txt` changed; those and `continue_game.json` were put back from the backups, and both save folders and the three settings files compared equal to them. No crash folder was made. The framework was uninstalled, which put back the earlier `version.dll`; the backups and screenshots were deleted.
- **Changed after the run, not run in the game**: the branch was rebased onto the merges of C9, then of C10 and C3, then of C4, which put `GameApi::country` after C4's `console_run`; that build, with 411 dependency points, passed the build, the tests and the offline checks against `eu4.exe`, and was never started in the game. After the run, too, the ABI changed: `GameApi::country` now writes the snapshot as JSON (ADR snapshots-cross-the-abi-as-json), war names included, where the run's build had `abi::Country` and `abi::CountryLink`s, which no extension called in the run either; and a country's snapshot reads only its own wars in full, so another war with a tag that is no country's, or one that cannot be read, no longer fails it. Unit tests cover each.

## 2026-10-29 Time (remote control C1, tested by me)

Every request went through `eu4-harmony game`, built from this branch; `eu4.exe` was started directly. Chinese playset with the unchanged regional-combat extension, system fonts; backups as above. What the screen showed was seen with `tools/screenshot.ps1`, and the player's input sent with `tools/game-input.ps1`. Two runs: the second after the fix for game events below, with the saves put back in between.

- **Startup**: 386 dependency points match (the new ones keep the speed setter and the speed-up command, the pause toggle, the map manager's popups and the event window's class, and the restart); 103 hooks (the new one observes the restart; the daily update's observer gained an `after`), 4 writes.
- **Main menu**: `time` printed `not_in_game: the main menu is open`, exit code 1.
- **The clock**: the continue box loaded the Castile save of 1470.3.13; `time` read 1470.3.13, paused, speed 4. `time speed 2` and then `time speed 5` answered null, `time` read them back, and the speed dial in the top bar showed two and then five segments lit. `time speed 6` was refused, `invalid_argument`. At speed 1, `time resume` and 4.5 s later `time pause` took the date to 1470.3.15 (two days of 2 s), and `time` read paused; a second `time pause` answered null.
- **Advancing 30 days**: `time advance 30 --speed 5` from 1470.3.15 answered after 3.7 s: `stopped` `date`, 1470.4.14, the 30 day events 1470.3.16 to 1470.4.14 (numbers 4 to 33), `missed` false. `time` read 1470.4.14, paused, speed 5, and the top bar showed 1470年4月14日 and the pause button. In the second run, `time advance 30 --speed 1` from 1470.10.2 answered after 58.6 s with 1470.11.1, its 30 day events among 38 events (the others the player's armies' orders), and the game stood at 1470.11.1, paused.
- **The player pausing**: 5 s into `time advance 60 --speed 2`, the space key was sent to the game; the advance answered at once `stopped` `paused`, "the game was paused before the date, by the player, an extension or another remote client", at 1470.4.19, with the day events 4.15 to 4.19.
- **A game event**: `time advance 365 --speed 5` from 1470.4.19 answered after 17.8 s `stopped` `game_event`, "a game event waits for the player's answer", at 1470.9.22, with 164 events (day events and the player's armies' orders and defeats in the war with the Ottomans); the screen showed the event 商人们的苦难 with its two options, and the game paused. But `time` then read 1470.9.23: the game had gone on a day after the advance paused it at the safe point where it found the event window. Fixed: the end of the daily update now pauses the game as an event window opens during an advance. In the second run the same advance answered after 19.5 s at 1470.9.22, with the event 巴斯克群众的支持率提高 on the screen, and `time` read 1470.9.22, paused, also a second later.
- **An event waiting already**: `time advance 1` with the event's window open answered in 0.07 s `game_event`, no events, without letting time run. `time resume` then ran the game with the window still open (1470.9.24); after the event's option was clicked, `time advance 2 --speed 3` ran to its date, and in the second run `time advance 1` from 1470.9.22 to 9.23.
- **Giving up**: `time advance 365 --speed 1 --timeout 3` ended after 3.0 s with exit code 3; `time` read 1470.9.29 running, and 3 s later 1470.9.30 paused: the advance called off stopped time at the end of the day under way. A command line stopped from outside while its advance ran (a PowerShell job ended with its shell) gave the same: "given up by its client while it waited" in the log, and the game paused at the end of that day.
- **Windows**: with the in-game menu open, and then with its "return to the main menu?" confirmation open, an advance resumed the game and time ran (1470.9.24 to 9.26): neither holds the game paused once resumed. The end-of-game summary does not either: an advance started on it ran from 1470.9.27 to 10.2.
- **Returning to the main menu**: with that advance running on the summary, the summary's exit button was clicked. The log has "the game starts eu4.exe again" 0.1 s later, and in the same millisecond `time.advance: ok as the process ends, after 11414 ms`; the transport logged the client closing the pipe after its one answer 4 ms after that. The command line printed `stopped` `main_menu`, "the game is returning to the main menu: eu4.exe starts again", 1470.10.2, with the day events 9.28 to 10.2, exit code 0. The new process started 0.3 s after the answer, found the pipe still held, opened it 3 s later, and answered `time` with `not_in_game` at its main menu.
- **Costs**: every time request that answers at once took under 0.05 ms in the engine. The core logs of the three processes have no error, and no extension was stopped.
- **Not seen in the running game**: another game loaded or started during an advance (`session`): the in-game menu's load button is disabled for this save, and C5's loading is built at the same time (unit test with a fake game); a window holding the game paused during an advance, such as a message set to pause it (unit test); the day event waited for past 2 s (unit test); more than 10,000 events during one advance (unit test); quitting to the desktop during an advance, where the client finds the pipe broken; an extension pausing, resuming or setting the speed, since none does (the SDK's tests call them through a capability table built as the core builds its own, and through an older core's table that ends before them).
- **Cleanup**: no save was added; the saves the runs rewrote and the settings files were put back from the backups, and both save folders and the settings files compared equal to them. The game was ended from outside (its process stopped) after each run, and no crash folder was made. The framework was uninstalled, which put back the earlier `version.dll`; the backups and screenshots were deleted.
- **Changed after the run, not run in the game**: the end of the daily update and the advance's check at a safe point ask one function whether a game event waits, which reads what the run's build read; a stop goes with its session, so an advance given up while a save loads, or one still held when another game is loaded, never pauses the new game; an advance that answers an error lets its stop go instead of calling it off. That the game stays at an advance's speed afterwards, as the run saw (speed 5 after `--speed 5`), is now said in the capability's description. Unit tests cover each.

A third run followed the second review, with the build of `6f9ecd7` (time's names in `objects`, the player's own pause wherever an advance stops, a given-up advance's stop let go once the game paused), from before the branch was rebased onto the merges of C4 and C2 (the same changes are now `c854a7e`, `2f974b7` and `2d858ce`), installed with `dev-install.ps1` and the command line of the same build; `eu4.exe` started directly, the same playset, the saves and settings backed up as before.

- **Startup**: 403 dependency points match (the merged nodes' points included), 103 hooks, 4 writes; the core log has no error.
- **Pause on events**: the continue box loaded the Castile save of 1470.3.13. In the in-game settings, 触发事件时暂停 (pause on events; `autosave_on_events` in `settings.txt`) was ticked and applied, so a game event's window holds the game paused by itself.
- **The window alone, without an advance**: `time resume` at speed 5 ran to 1470.12.21, where the game event 士兵不学无术 came up and the game paused. Its option was clicked and time ran on, to 1470.12.29, where the next game event, 狂妄的官员, paused it: the game event's window had held the game alone, as it did under an advance before this fix.
- **`time pause` on a window's pause**: at 1470.12.29, with only that game event's window holding the game, `time pause` answered null; its option was then clicked, and `time` read 1470.12.29, paused, five times over 4 s, the window gone.
- **An advance stopped by a game event**: `time advance 365 --speed 5` from 1470.12.29 answered after 2.4 s `game_event`, "a game event waits for the player's answer", at 1471.1.13, with the 15 day events 12.30 to 1.13; the screen showed the game event 患难之交. `time` read 1471.1.13 paused for 3 s. Its first option was clicked by hand, and `time` read 1471.1.13, paused, six times over 5 s after, the window gone: the game no longer runs on once the game event is answered.
- **Plain advances**: `time advance 30 --speed 5` from 1470.3.13 answered after 1.8 s `date`, 1470.4.12, with the 30 day events 3.14 to 4.12, and `time` read 1470.4.12 paused, also 3 s later. After the game events, `time advance 20 --speed 5` from 1471.1.13 answered `date` at 1471.2.2 and `time advance 20 --speed 3` `date` at 1471.2.22 (10.5 s), each with its 20 day events, and the game stood on those dates. `time speed 1`, `time resume` and `time pause` then ran the game a day and paused it.
- **Not seen in this run**: a message set to pause that opens on the stop date, and an advance answering `paused` while only a window holds the game (unit tests; they pause through the same function the game event's case ran); an advance given up within the frame after the game paused, which cannot be timed by hand, since the transport notices a client gone within 50 ms and the gap is one frame (unit test); whether letting the windows go, which pausing on a window's pause does and the player's own pause command does not, matters elsewhere in the game (nothing was seen).
- **Cleanup**: the run added no save. `Castilla-5.eu4` and its backup, which the half-year autosave rewrote at 1471.1.1, and `settings.txt`, where applying the settings also wrote `rich_presence=no`, rounded volumes and `graceful_exit`, were put back from the backups, and both save folders and the settings files compared equal to them. Applying the settings also rewrote `pdx_settings.txt` and `shortcuts.txt`, and the start rewrote `messagetypes_custom.txt`, as every start does; AGENTS.md does not list them, they were not backed up and were not compared. The game's process was stopped from outside; no crash folder was made. The framework was uninstalled, which put back the earlier `version.dll` (its hash as before); the backups and screenshots were deleted.

A fourth run repeated the checks with the build of `0327405`, the branch rebased onto the merges of C4 and C2, the same way:

- **Startup**: 420 dependency points match (C4's and C2's included), 103 hooks, 4 writes; the core log has no error.
- **Plain advances**: with pause on events ticked again, `time advance 30 --speed 5` from 1470.3.13 answered after 3.4 s `date` at 1470.4.12 with its 30 day events, and the game stood there. At the end, `time advance 10 --speed 3` from 1470.9.22 answered `date` at 1470.10.2 with its 10 day events, and the game stood there.
- **An advance stopped by a game event**: `time advance 365 --speed 5` answered after 20.8 s `game_event` at 1470.9.22, with its 163 day events among 171 events; the screen showed the game event 贿赂泛滥. Its option was clicked by hand, and `time` read 1470.9.22, paused, six times over 5 s. Then every message popup still open (a revolt, occupations, a spy network found, cardinals) was closed one by one, and `time` still read 1470.9.22, paused, with nothing on the screen: the player's own pause held the game.
- **Cleanup**: `pdx_settings.txt`, `shortcuts.txt` and `messagetypes_custom.txt` were backed up too this time. Applying the settings left `pdx_settings.txt` as it was and wrote `shortcuts.txt` with the same bindings in another order, which suggests the third run's rewrite of them changed nothing either. Everything backed up, both save folders included, was put back and compared equal; no save was added and no crash folder made; the framework was uninstalled, the earlier `version.dll` back with its hash, and the backups and screenshots deleted.

## 2026-10-29 Recruiting (remote control C6, tested by me)

Two runs. The first, of two starts, used `eu4-harmony game recruit` and the core built from this branch before it was rebased onto C4, with the `trace` feature (`EU4HARMONY_TRACE` set to the recruit command's check `0x26e080` and its execute `0x26cfe0`, 60 calls each) and two log lines of the run's own around the post to the session: they read the session's kind, `+0x54`, `+0x55` and `+0x80d` and the player mode, and the build set `+0x80d` around the post, as the map manager's frame does (removed afterwards, see below). For the extension side, a probe of the run's own: a mod made with `eu4-harmony new` in a scratch folder whose `on_session` recruits through `Game::recruit` and logs each answer, linked and added to `dlc_load.json` for the run only. `eu4.exe` was started directly; Chinese playset with the unchanged regional-combat extension, system fonts; backups as above, `dlc_load.json` too. What the game showed was seen with `eu4-harmony game screenshot` and `tools/screenshot.ps1`.

- **Startup**: 409 dependency points match; 104 hooks (two of them the trace's), 4 writes; both extensions loaded.
- **Main menu**: `recruit 219 infantry` answered `not_in_game: the main menu is open`, exit code 1.
- **Castile, 1470.3.13** (the continue box's ironman save), never unpaused at first. The probe's orders at the session's first frame: a galley in Seville (224) `Ok(())`; infantry in Toledo (219) `Rejected` "没有足够的manpower。"; artillery in Toledo `Rejected` "the player has no unit type of the kind artillery"; cavalry in Constantinople (151) `Rejected` "卡斯蒂利亚不是君士坦丁堡的控制者。\n没有足够的manpower。", one reason a line; `UnitKind::Unknown` `InvalidArgument` from the SDK; province 0 `NotFound`. From the command line the same refusals, exit code 1; 0 and 99999 `not_found`; `recruit 224 transport` printed `null`, exit code 0. Seville's province window then counted 0 regiments and 2 ships in its queue: the probe's galley and the cog.
- **Manpower**: Castile had none, and two years at speed 5 (to 1472.6.5) gave none, its armies at war taking every man back as they reinforced; the game console, opened with its key, refused `manpower 20000` "Command not available in multiplayer or ironman mode." So the game was ended, the France save of 1444.11.11 was made the newest save (its modification time) so that the continue box offered it, and the game was started again.
- **France, 1444.11.11**: `province.get 183` (Paris) allowed infantry and cavalry. Paused, `recruit 183 infantry` printed `null`; the outliner's list of units being recruited showed "50d 巴黎", its tooltip "我们正在巴黎招募拉丁中世纪步兵。". `recruit 183 cavalry`, still paused: the top bar went from 585 ducats and 10,798 men to 560 and 9,798. Running, on 1444.11.16, `recruit 183 infantry` printed `null`; paused again on 11.25, the outliner showed "36d (+2)" in Paris. Refused: `183 heavy_ship` "没有足够的sailors。"; `183 transport` "the game does not let the player recruit it there, and gives no reason" (Paris is inland); `219 infantry` "法兰西不是托雷多的控制者。"; `183 artillery` no unit type. The probe's orders, for Castile's provinces, were refused as not France's.
- **The game carrying it out**: for every order the trace had the framework's call of the check, and at the next frame, 2 to 9 ms later, the session's own check of a copy of the command (from `0x14cab33`) and its execute (from `0x14d4e62`), both answering 1, paused and running alike.
- **The session**: at every order, in both saves, of kind 2, `+0x54` 0, `+0x55` 1, `+0x80d` already 1, player mode 0, and the post took the command. Setting `+0x80d` around the post so changed nothing; it was taken out of the code, with its two dependency points, before the second run.
- **Costs**: each `recruit` took 0.1 to 0.2 ms in the engine; the probe's six orders in `on_session` 15 to 19 ms. The core logs have no error, and no extension was stopped.

The second run used the final build of this branch, rebased onto C4, where `recruit` comes after `console_run` in `GameApi`, without the trace or the run's own log lines, and the probe rebuilt with its SDK, now ordering for France: infantry and a transport in Paris, infantry in Toledo, `UnitKind::Unknown`, province 0. The France save was made the newest again.

- **Startup**: 408 dependency points match; 102 hooks, 4 writes; both extensions loaded. `recruit 183 infantry` in the main menu: `not_in_game`.
- **The extension**: infantry in Paris `Ok(())`; the transport "the game does not let the player recruit it there, and gives no reason"; Toledo "法兰西不是托雷多的控制者。"; `Unknown` `InvalidArgument`; 0 `NotFound` (18 ms for the five).
- **The command line**: paused, after the probe's regiment, the top bar read 585 ducats and 10,798 men, as after the first run's first regiment; `recruit 183 cavalry` printed `null`, and the top bar read 560 and 9,798; the outliner showed "50d (+1)" in Paris. `recruit 183 heavy_ship` "没有足够的sailors。".
- **Not seen in the running game**: the command's own rule for ships refusing an order the recruit check allows ("the game's recruit command refuses it, and gives no reason"; the restriction `0x40d2b0` was not met); a session that is not a client (a multiplayer host), which would take the order only within the map manager's frame and otherwise be answered "the game takes no orders from the player now"; a refusal for money or a siege; the player mode 7; an order the game's own check refuses at the next frame, the state having changed in between; special regiments and mercenaries, which `recruit` does not offer.
- **Cleanup**: the ironman save `Castilla-5.eu4` and its backup were rewritten while the game ran, the France save changed (its modification time by hand), and so did `settings.txt`. Those were put back from the backups, and both save folders, the three settings files, `continue_game.json` and `dlc_load.json` compared equal to them after each run. The game was ended from outside (its process stopped) each time; no crash folder was made. The link file `mod\eu4-harmony-recruit-probe.mod` and the probe's logs were deleted. The framework was uninstalled, which put back the earlier `version.dll`; the backups, the probe mod, the screenshots and the copies of the logs were deleted.
- **Changed after the run, not run in the game**: the second run's build was this branch rebased onto C4's merge, at the commit that was then 676e229 ("Write down in the plan what recruiting gives"; its code was that of "Post the recruit command as the button does, without the frame flag", two commits before, since the two only wrote documents). Since then the branch was rebased onto the merge of C2, which put `recruit` behind the country snapshot's field in `GameApi` (the SDK's tests check its place against a table built as the core builds its own), and, after review: the recruit command's class, size and methods are named in `objects::campaign`, and the allocator is `unicode::fill`'s, with the same numbers and bytes and one dependency point fewer (the allocator was kept twice); the session is found before the command is allocated, where the run's build found it after the command's check, so that a session that cannot be read leaves no command behind; `FakeGame` answers a recruit order as the core does; and the docs of `recruit` say that the game checks an order again at its next frame and name the ship rule a province's snapshot leaves out. On the path that ran, the game sees the same calls, but the map manager's method `0x811aa0` now comes before the allocation, and is called for an order the command's check refuses too. That build, with 421 dependency points, passed the build, the tests and the offline checks against `eu4.exe`, and was never started in the game.
- **Rebased onto C1, not run in the game**: the branch was then rebased onto the merge of C1, which put `recruit` behind time control's four fields (`time_speed`, `time_set_speed`, `time_pause` and `time_resume`) in `GameApi`, where it had come right after `country`; that build, with 430 dependency points (C1's included), passed the build, the tests and the offline checks against `eu4.exe`, and was never started in the game.

## 2026-10-29 Questions and offers (remote control C7, tested by me)

Three runs. The first used the build of this branch from before its rebase onto the merges of C10 and C3, with 407 dependency points; the second and third, after the review, are below. In the first, every request went through `eu4-harmony game` of that build; `eu4.exe` was started directly. Chinese playset with the unchanged regional-combat extension, system fonts; backups as above. The screen was seen with `tools/screenshot.ps1`, the game's own log (`logs/game.log`) was read for what the event windows logged, and the speed and the pause were set with `tools/game-input.ps1` (the + key and Space).

- **Startup**: 407 dependency points match (the questions' and offers' among them); 102 hooks, 4 writes.
- **Into a game**: the continue box loaded the Castile save of 1470.3.13. It is an ironman save: the game console, opened with the key left of 1 (sent by a scratch script, since `tools/game-input.ps1` has no key for it), answered `event flavor_fra.109` with "Command not available in multiplayer or ironman mode". So no event was raised by hand; they came as time ran at the fastest speed. `question.list` and `offer.list` read `[]` at first.
- **A question**: at 1470.6.1 `question.list` read `army_professionalism_events.1`, id 20378, deadline 1470.9.28, with the title "Kostantiniyye之劫", the two-paragraph description and the options 0 "改革军饷、严明军纪！", 1 "严惩作奸犯科之人！" and 2 "这就是战争的规则。", as the event window on screen showed them. Paused, option 7 answered `invalid_argument` and question 99999 `not_found` (exit code 1 each); option 2 answered null: the window closed, the list was empty, the game's log has "Selected event option 2 for event army_professionalism_events.1", and the top bar's prestige went from 80 to 70, the option's −10, while the game stayed paused.
- **With the game running**: `catholic_flavor.4` ("买卖圣职？", two options) was listed at 1472.1.22 and answered with option 0 at once; the list was empty a day later and the game's log has "Selected event option 0 for event catholic_flavor.4". Two questions left unanswered meanwhile were answered by the game past their deadline ("Auto-selected event option 0 for event 5005", "… ideagroups.1809").
- **Offers**: `offer.list` read a royal marriage from `POL` (id 40, made 1470.10.15, listed 1470.10.18), title "王室联姻" and the dialog's description, which says legitimacy would drop by 5. Paused, `offer.accept --offer 41` answered `not_found`; offer 40 null, the list was empty, legitimacy went from 93 to 88 at once, and accepting 40 again answered `not_found`. A royal marriage from `HAB` (id 42, 1471.5.13) had its icon among the message icons at the top left, beside the Ottomans' peace offer's; `offer.decline 42` answered null, its icon went and the Ottomans' stayed, and the list then held that peace offer (id 43, "提议和平", 无条件和平). `offer.accept 43` answered null and `country.hostile CAS TUR`, true before, read false, still paused.
- **Costs**: 460 lists took at most 0.5 ms (0.04 ms on average), each answer at most 0.5 ms. The core log has no error, and no extension was stopped.
- **Not seen in the running game**: an option the player may not choose (no such event came, and the console could not raise one; the engine's refusal is a unit test, and the layer's check was only seen letting every option through); an offer answered from a dialog open on screen rather than from its icon (every offer of the run was an icon); a window whose pending event went before it was answered; an extension reading or answering them, since none does (the SDK's and the core's tests read and write the same JSON); the game set to pause on events: the run's settings did not pause on events, so no event window held the game paused, and that answering a question lets go of such a hold, as a click does, is read from the code, not seen.
- **Changed after the run, not run in the game**: the texts now go through `game_text::plain`, which the province snapshot (C3) brought, so an icon in them reads as its name, where the run's build only left colors out (none of the run's texts had an icon); the branch was then rebased onto the screenshot (C10) and the province snapshot (C3).
- **Cleanup**: the run rewrote the ironman save; both save folders and the three settings files were put back from the backups, which were then deleted, not compared first. The game was ended from outside (its process stopped), and no crash folder was made. The framework was uninstalled, which put back the earlier `version.dll`; the screenshots and scratch scripts were deleted.

The second run, after the review, used the build of `53537ab`: this branch rebased onto the merge of C2, with the review's fixes up to the move of the names into `objects` (after the rebases onto the merges of C1 and C6 the same changes are `6a66e44` to `373814c`): offers older than two months left out, an answer that asks the dialog's own check first, only diplomacy dialogs about an action taken for offers. `eu4.exe` was started directly with `-start_tag=TUR`, a new game as the Ottomans in normal mode, no save loaded; the same playset and fonts; the saves and the settings files backed up as AGENTS.md says, with `dlc_load.json`, `messagetypes_custom.txt`, `pdx_settings.txt`, `shortcuts.txt` and `gameplaysettings.txt`. The screen was seen with `eu4-harmony game screenshot`; the speed was set with the top bar's buttons and the minus key, and the pause with Space (`tools/game-input.ps1`).

- **Startup**: 442 dependency points match; 102 hooks, 4 writes.
- **A question, the game running**: at 1444.11.14 `question.list` read `flavor_tur.203` (id 152, "大破瓦尔纳十字军", one option). Option 5 answered `invalid_argument`; option 0 null, the list was empty, and the game's log has "Selected event option 0 for event flavor_tur.203".
- **An offer past its two months**: France asked for military access (id 3, "索要军事通行权", made 1453.3.21; the game was paused at 1453.4.2 as it was listed). At speed 1, a day at a time: at 1453.5.21 `offer.list` listed it; at 1453.5.22, two months and a day after it was made, the list was empty while its icon still showed among the message icons at the top left, faded as an icon flashes. `offer.accept --offer 3` answered `rejected`, "the game no longer takes an answer to offer 3, and has let it go" (0.1 ms in the engine); in the next screenshot the icon was gone, and accepting again answered `not_found`. The game stayed paused at 1453.5.22 throughout.
- **An offer in time**: Tunis proposed an alliance (id 4, "提议结盟", made 1455.7.3; paused at 1455.7.17). `offer.accept --offer 4` answered null, the list was empty, and `country.get TUR`, whose relations were empty before, read `ally` TUN.

- **Not seen in the running game**: the game declining France's offer when the check let it go (that is the game's own check, read from its code; France's side was not looked at); a dialog's check saying no for another reason than its age; everything the first run did not see, as listed there.
- **Cleanup**: the run added no save; the three autosaves it rotated, `continue_game.json`, `settings.txt`, `messagetypes_custom.txt`, `pdx_settings.txt` and `shortcuts.txt` were put back from the backups, and everything backed up, both save folders included, compared equal to them. `pdx_login_cache.txt` and `pops_filestorage.index`, which every start rewrites, were not backed up. The game's process was stopped from outside; no crash folder was made. The framework was uninstalled, which put back the earlier `version.dll` (its hash as before); the backups and screenshots were deleted.

The third run used the build of `61b9947`: the branch rebased onto the merge of C1, where an advance now stops on exactly the questions `question.list` lists (`questions::count`) and its stop reason is `question`, the glossary's word, where C1's runs above saw `game_event`. The branch was then rebased onto the merge of C6, which put the questions' and offers' fields in `GameApi` behind C6's `recruit` (the same changes are now `6a66e44` to `b3ea769`); that build passed the build, the tests and the offline checks against `eu4.exe`, and was not run in the game. Started and backed up as the second run, from a new game as the Ottomans.

- **Startup**: 449 dependency points match; 103 hooks, 4 writes. The core log has no error.
- **An advance stopped by a question**: `time` read 1444.11.11, paused. `time advance 365 --speed 5` answered after 1.6 s at 1444.11.14 (what it printed for `stopped` was cut off in the run's output), and `question.list` read `flavor_tur.203` (id 151). `question.answer --question 151 --option 0` answered null, the list was empty, the game's log has its "Selected event option 0", and `time` read 1444.11.14, paused, six times over 6 s. A second `time advance 365 --speed 5` answered `stopped` `question`, "a question waits for the player's answer", at 1445.2.22, with its 100 day events; the event window 内部权力结构 was on the screen and `question.list` read `flavor_tur.206` (id 509). Answered with option 0, null; `time` read 1445.2.22, paused, five times over 5 s, and the list was empty.
- **An offer**: a royal marriage from Dulkadir (id 1, made 1444.12.28) was listed at 1445.2.22; `offer.decline --offer 1` answered null, the list was empty, the game still paused.
- **Not seen in the running game**: the game set to pause on events, here too: the in-game menu was not reached past the message popups open, so no event window held the game paused, and the advance's double toggle under such a window, followed by an answer through `question.answer`, was not seen (C1's third and fourth runs saw it with the option clicked by hand); the expired offer and the alliance of the second run, not repeated with this build.

## 2026-10-29 The main menu after review (remote control C5, tested by me)

The final build: this branch at `Refuse a load the game's own loader could not read`, rebased onto C6's merge, with the review's changes and C1's restart reconciled (452 dependency points match; 103 hooks, 4 writes). An attempt earlier the same day with the build before its last two changes, on C1's merge (442 points), found the two problems those changes fix; it is at the end. Every request went through `eu4-harmony game`; `eu4.exe` was started directly, the single player setup's buttons clicked with `tools/game-input.ps1`. Chinese playset with the unchanged regional-combat extension, system fonts; backups as AGENTS.md says. Two test saves were put in the save games folder: `c5-broken-meta`, a copy of 法兰西1445_02_20 with one byte of its `meta` entry flipped, and `c5-damaged`, the same with 64 KiB in the middle of its `gamestate` entry zeroed, so that it no longer inflates.

- **The main menu**: `menu` said `starting`, then `main_menu` 3 s later. `menu.saves` listed 141 saves, the test saves first (`c5-damaged` FRA 1445.2.20; `c5-broken-meta` with a null player and date); `session` was `not_in_game: the main menu is open`, `menu.return` `not_in_game: no game is going on`, `menu.load c5-broken-meta` `rejected: the save cannot be read: zip entry of the wrong size`, `menu.start QQQ` `not_found`.
- **A page the player chose**: with the single player setup entered by hand, `menu` said `main_menu` (no start made in it), `menu.load` and `menu.start` were `unavailable: only from the main menu's first page`, and a screenshot after them still showed the setup.
- **A return the player made**: the setup's back button starts `eu4.exe` again, as returning to the main menu does. An `events` read waiting (`--wait-ms 60000`) when it was clicked was answered `not_ready` ("the game is returning to the main menu ...") 2.3 s after it was sent, at the restart, and a `menu` sent after it at once; the new process answered 45 s later.
- **A save the game cannot read**: `menu.load c5-damaged` was answered after 0.9 s, `rejected: the game could not read the save: it starts a game anyway, from what it read of it`. A second client's `session`, sent 0.5 s after the load, waited and was answered at the game's first frame: session 2, 1445.2.20, `FRA`. From the next request on, `session` and `time` were `not_in_game: no controllable player`, and `menu` said `game`.
- **Returning without a country to play**: there, `menu.return` answered `{"place": "ending"}`, and `menu` 0.3 s later `not_ready`. Again after a new game as France switched to spectating with `console run observe` ("Entered Spectator Mode.", then `session` `no controllable player`): `menu.return` answered `ending`, and an `events` read waiting since the last event was answered `not_ready` 0.55 s after it was sent.
- **A load**: `menu.load 阿尔达比勒1454_09_13` (a compressed text save made with the playset's mods) answered after 4.2 s: session 2, 1454.9.13, `ARL`, the save's header; a second client's `menu` said `starting`, and its `session` waited and read the same.
- **An advance when returning**: `time advance 365 --speed 1`, an `events` read waiting since the last event, then `menu.return`: the advance answered `stopped: main_menu` at 1454.9.14, with that day's event and `process`; the events read `not_ready`.
- **A start**: `menu.start FRA` answered after 6.4 s: session 1, 1444.11.11, `FRA`. A `menu` sent 0.5 s after it waited 5.6 s for the main thread to present a frame and said `starting`.
- **A start made by hand**: in the setup, France selected and its start button clicked, the game asked for the game mode; until that was confirmed `menu` said `main_menu` and `session` `the main menu is open`, as no start was made yet. Once confirmed, `menu` said `starting` (answered 3.9 s later, at the next frame presented), then `game`, and `session` read session 1, `FRA`.
- **A country with no land at the start**: `menu.start PRU` (Prussia) answered after 8.2 s with session 1, 1444.11.11, `PRU`; from the next request on, `session` and `time` were `not_in_game: no controllable player`. `menu.start` does not check that the country exists at the start date.
- **A save made with a mod the playset lacks**: `menu.load 符腾堡1445_07_19`, made with a gameplay mod not in the playset, crashed the game 2.8 s after it ran, after the save loader had returned, in the load command the main menu's frame ran (`eu4+0x88a1fb`, reading past the end of an array of the game's; research/main-menu.md). `menu.saves` does not say which mods a save was made with, and `menu.load` does not compare them with the playset.
- **Costs**: requests answered at once took under 1 ms, but the first `menu.saves`, 10.8 ms for 141 saves (in the attempt below).
- **Not seen in the running game**: a load or start given up after 30 s with no step (no start the game did not take up came about); `rejected` because the setup did not take a start; a page the player changes between a load's request and the main menu's frame that runs it (only the refusal on a page already changed was seen); a load whose first session has another player or date than its header, since the damaged save was refused by the loader check first (unit test); an ironman save with this build (the earlier runs loaded one).
- **The attempt before**, with the build on C1's merge: the main menu, a page the player chose and the return by hand went as above. `menu.load c5-damaged` was answered with session 2, 1445.2.20, `FRA`: the loader had failed (`saves: load did not establish the current world`), the header comparison let it through, and the game had no controllable player. And the second client's `session`, sent 0.5 s after that load, was answered `the main menu is open` 3 s later, at the frame presented just before the game's first: the frame the game took over in, where the main menu's frame still ran and none of the start's flags read as set. The final build fixes both: the loader's own result, and the main menu counted open only while it is the world's idler. That the second fixes it was seen only in that the final run's second clients waited, three times; the first attempt's false answer came from one frame and was not provoked again.
- **Cleanup**: the game crashed once, at the load above; its crash folder was deleted. Otherwise it was closed by ending its process. The two test saves were deleted and `settings.txt` put back; both save folders, the three settings files, `continue_game.json` and `dlc_load.json` compared equal to the backup. The framework was uninstalled, which put back the earlier `version.dll`; the backups, logs kept for the record and screenshots were deleted.
- **Rebased onto C7, not run in the game**: the branch was then rebased onto the merge of C7, which renamed C1's stop reason `game_event` to `question` (`Stop::Question`, `questions_waiting`, counted by `questions::count`, the questions `question.list` lists). The rebase kept both: C7's names and counting, and this branch's restart path (C1's restart observer answers every request through `Engine::end`) and `process` in C1's event reads and in an advance's answer. That build, with 482 dependency points, passed the build, the tests and the offline checks against `eu4.exe`, and was never started in the game.

## 2026-10-29 Diplomacy (remote control C8, tested by me)

`eu4-harmony game diplomacy ...` built from this branch at its third commit (the capability layer, the engine and the ABI, before the peace offer's own "not at war" refusal), and for the extension side a throwaway probe made with `eu4-harmony new` in a scratch folder, linked and added to `dlc_load.json` for the run: on its first `on_day` it listed the war goals against the owner of Serbia (141), proposed an alliance to the owner of Marash (332), offered that country peace and declared war on the first, then asked `diplomacy_answer` each frame until it was no longer pending. `eu4.exe` was started directly with `-start_tag=TUR` (a new game as the Ottomans, normal mode, 1444.11.11), the provinces in its environment; Chinese playset with the unchanged regional-combat extension, system fonts. Backups as AGENTS.md says, `dlc_load.json` too. Time was run on with the space bar and the speed keys (`tools/game-input.ps1`), C1 not being merged.

- **Startup**: 475 dependency points match; 103 hooks (the new one observes the game carrying out a diplomatic action's command), 4 writes; both extensions loaded.
- **War goals**: against Byzantium, Karaman, Serbia, Athens, Wallachia, Dulkadir and Candar only `{"casus_belli": "none", "war_goal": "nowargoal", "name": "无战争目标"}`; against Albania `cb_core`/`take_core` for Lezhe (4175, "收回核心省份莱什") and Kruje (4750, "收回核心省份克鲁耶"), then `none`. About 1 ms each in the engine. Not compared with the declare war window on screen.
- **Alliances**: Byzantium refused at once, `rejected` with the game's two reasons, "除非我国对拜占庭有正面看法（当前为-45），否则无法与其结盟。" and "拜占庭不喜欢我国，无法提议结盟。他们对我们必须有正面的看法，当前为-95。", the description the check writes after them left out; Karaman `{"answer": "declined"}` after 89 ms; a second offer the same day, to Dulkadir, `rejected`: "你必须等待一天才可以提出另一个结盟提议。". On the probe's day the probe's offer to Dulkadir came back `Accepted` 3 frames after it was sent, and `country.get TUR` then had `ally DUL`.
- **Declaring war** on Albania for Kruje: `rejected` "在1444年12月11日之前不能宣战。" on the first day (the game's first month), and the same from the probe for Serbia; on 1444.12.13 `{"answer": "declared", "war": "奥斯曼收复克鲁耶之战"}` after 43 ms, and `country.get TUR` then had that war, the Ottomans attacking, Albania, Venice and Naxos defending. Declaring again: `rejected` with three reasons, the diplomat's wait until 1445.1.14, "他们正与我们交战。" and the month after the last war began.
- **Peace**: offered to Byzantium, not at war, `rejected` "the game does not allow it, and gives no reason" (the build after the run says the player is not at war with them instead); to Albania during the diplomat's wait, `rejected` with it; to Venice, a separate peace, `declined` (91 ms); a second offer the same day, to Naxos, `rejected` "你必须等待一天才可以提出另一个和平提议。" and "附庸国或被联合统治的国家无法签订和平条约。"; to Albania on 1445.1.17 `declined`. After the game console's `winwars` and one more day, Venice `accepted` (136 ms), and the war's defenders were Albania alone.
- **The probe** also read `NotFound` "no diplomatic action 9999 is kept" for a ticket never given, and the peace offer to a country it was not at war with refused as above; its `on_day` took 63 ms, the two province lists most of it.
- **Costs**: 1 to 1.5 ms in the engine for a check or a refusal; the answers came 43 to 136 ms after the request ran, the game paused or running at speed 5. The core log has no error, and no extension was stopped.
- **Not seen in the running game**: an extension's declaration that began a war (the probe's was refused for the first month); a `pending` answer, which needs a human player; a declaration the game carried out without a war beginning; Dulkadir, the Ottomans' ally, called into the war on Albania (it did not join, and whether it was called was not established); the declare war window compared with `diplomacy.war_goals`; a war goal about a country (`target`); the peace offer's "not at war" refusal, added after the run.
- **Cleanup**: the run rotated the autosaves and changed `settings.txt` and `continue_game.json`; the save folder, those two and `dlc_load.json` were put back from the backups, and both save folders and the four settings files compared equal to them. No crash folder was made. The game was closed by ending its process. The probe's link file and log were deleted; the framework was uninstalled, which put back the earlier `version.dll` (its hash as before the run); the backups, the probe and the screenshot were deleted.
- **Rebased onto C1 and C6, not run in the game**: the branch was then rebased onto the merges of C1 and C6, which put diplomacy's five fields in `GameApi` behind time control's four and `recruit`, where the run's build had them right after `country`, and gave the session's objects to both (the same file); that build passed the build, the tests and the offline checks against `eu4.exe`, and was never started in the game. The SDK's tests check the fields' places against a table built as the core builds its own.

## 2026-10-29 Diplomacy after review (remote control C8, tested by me)

The build: this branch at `Say at Engine::end that a waiting diplomatic action answers pending`, with the review's changes, rebased onto the requirement branch after C5's merge (566 dependency points match; 104 hooks, 4 writes; the regional-combat extension loaded). Every request went through `eu4-harmony game`; `eu4.exe` was started directly with `-start_tag=TUR` (a new game as the Ottomans, normal mode, 1444.11.11). Chinese playset, system fonts; backups as AGENTS.md says, `pdx_settings.txt` and `shortcuts.txt` too. The game ran 4 minutes; time went on only by `time.advance`, a day at speed 5 taking 0.12 to 0.28 s and 28 days 3.8 s.

- **Allies**: `country.get TUR` had no relation. Dulkadir, Candar and Georgia, asked a day apart, each `accepted` (37 to 138 ms), and `country.get TUR` then listed the three as allies. The game console's `trust <tag> 100` raised the Ottomans' trust with each ("Trust with 杜勒卡迪尔 changed by 100").
- **Refusals** on 1444.12.12, declaring on Albania for Kruje: `--call-allies QQQ` was `not_found`; `BYZ`, `invalid_argument` "BYZ is not among the allies the declare war window lists"; `DUL,CND` `rejected` with the call box's tooltip for Candar: "the declare war window does not let the player call CND to arms:", then "估计他们不会接受。", for 55 and against -1107, trust +50, the Ottomans' reputation +5, "詹达尔没有欠奥斯曼至少10点人情：-1000" and "距离遥远的战争：-107". Nothing was sent: `country.get TUR` had no war. The game console's `favors <tag> 30` then gave the Ottomans favors with each ally.
- **A chosen ally**: on Karaman, which rivals Dulkadir and Candar, `--casus-belli none --call-allies DUL`: `{"answer": "declared", "called": ["DUL"], "war": "奥斯曼侵略战争"}` after 66 ms, and `country.get TUR` had that war with the Ottomans and Dulkadir attacking, Karaman defending. Two days later it was the same; Candar and Georgia were not in it and were still allies.
- **Every ally the window allows**: on Trebizond on 1445.1.13, at stability -3 after the war without a casus belli, `rejected` "你不能在国家过于混乱的时候宣战。必须先把稳定度提高到-3以上。"; after the game console's `stability 6` (3), with no `--call-allies`: `{"answer": "declared", "called": ["CND", "DUL"], "war": "2nd 奥斯曼侵略战争"}` after 84 ms; attackers the Ottomans, Candar and Dulkadir, defenders Trebizond, Georgia and Samtskhe. Georgia, Trebizond's ally too, was passed over, joined the defenders and was no longer the Ottomans' ally.
- **No ally**: on Byzantium on 1445.2.14, `--call-allies none`: `{"answer": "declared", "called": [], "war": "3rd 奥斯曼侵略战争"}` after 50 ms; the Ottomans attacking alone, Byzantium, Athens and Moldavia defending; two days later the same, Candar and Dulkadir still allies.
- **Costs**: about 1 ms in the engine for a war goal list or a refusal; the declarations were answered 50 to 84 ms after they ran. The core log has no error, and no extension was stopped.
- **Not seen in the running game**: an extension's declaration with calls (`GameApi::declare_war`'s `CALL_*`, `allies_called`), tried only in the SDK's and the core's tests; an ally named that joins anyway (a subject), or that the other side lists too (`ALLIED_TO_ENEMY`), refused by name: Georgia, in that case, was only passed over under `all`; an ally the check let the player call declining the call (every one called joined); the declare war window on screen compared with the allies called; a waiting action answered `pending` as the game returns to the main menu (unit test only); offering peace and proposing alliances beyond the three proposals above, which the review's changes touched only in their answer (unit tests).
- **An earlier run cut off**: a run with this round's build before the rebase (544 dependency points), at 12:26 the same day, was cut off by the machine's crash (bug check 0x133, the DPC watchdog; Windows restarted at 12:31). Its framework log, read before this run rotated it, ends at 12:28:50: three alliance proposals were answered only after 5.1 to 7.2 s, at or past the 5 s after which an action answers `pending`, and `time.advance 1 --speed 5` took 3.6 to 7.2 s, some twenty times as long as in this run; nothing in the log says why. The cleanup after it was done before this run, outside this branch: the framework uninstalled, the saves and settings put back and compared with that run's backup.
- **Cleanup**: the run rotated the autosaves and changed `settings.txt`, `continue_game.json` and `shortcuts.txt`; they were put back from the backup, and both save folders and the eight settings files backed up (`settings.txt`, `continue_game.json`, `pdx_settings.txt`, `shortcuts.txt`, `dlc_load.json`, `messagetypes_custom.txt`, `gameplaysettings.txt`, `eu4-harmony\settings.txt`), 150 files, compared equal to it. No crash folder was made. The game was closed by ending its process. ## 2026-10-29 Loose ends (remote control Z1, tested by me)

The build was this branch at `Give 676e229 its own subject in the recruiting run's record`, on the requirement branch as it was then (before C8's merge): 483 dependency points match; 103 hooks, 4 writes; the build, the tests and the offline checks passed first. Every request went through `eu4-harmony game` but two, sent by a PowerShell client of the run's own that reads its answer slowly (8 KiB every 10 ms; not kept); `eu4.exe` was started directly, the end-of-game summary's button clicked with `tools/game-input.ps1`. Chinese playset with the unchanged regional-combat extension, system fonts. Backups as AGENTS.md now says, `pdx_settings.txt` and `shortcuts.txt` included, and every other file at the top of the user folder and the framework's logs. Two test saves were made from 法兰西1445_02_20 (a compressed text save) with Python's `zipfile`: `z1-end-date`, its `meta` dated 1821.1.1, a day before the end date; and `z1-no-country`, its player `PRU` in `meta` and in `players_countries`. Four processes, one after another by returning to the main menu, as short as the checks; nothing ran at speed 5.

- **Mods the playset lacks**: `menu.saves` listed 141 saves, 26 with `missing_mods`: the Württemberg, Riga and Mazandaran saves `归墟之门`, an old autosave `EU4 Harmony Hello`; the test saves and 法兰西1445_02_20 none. `menu.load 符腾堡1445_07_19`, which crashed the game in C5's run, was answered at once `rejected: the save was made with mods the game did not start with: 归墟之门; loading it can crash the game. ...`, and `menu` still said `main_menu`.
- **A country with no land**: `menu.start PRU` was answered at once `rejected: PRU has no land at the start date, so it cannot be played from it; ...`. `menu.start FRA` then answered after 6.8 s with session 1.
- **A game console command of several words**: `console run cash 100`, unquoted, answered "Gold changed by 100", and France's treasury went from 66.441 to 166.441.
- **A load in a game**: `menu.load no-such-save` and `menu.load 法兰西1445_02_20` were both `unavailable: only in the main menu`.
- **The camera**, which now reads whether the map wraps: `camera look_at 5631 1000` looked at 5631, 1000 (province 1675); 5632 was `invalid_argument` (off the map, 5632 across); `camera zoom 10` `invalid_argument` (50 to 3000).
- **An advance when the player can no longer play**: in the third process, as France with its opening question answered, `time advance 30 --speed 1` ran (1444.11.12 after 3 s); `console run observe` from a second client answered "Entered Spectator Mode.", and the advance was answered 0.75 s later `not_in_game: the player can no longer play the game (no controllable player); time runs on as the game runs it`. (In the first process the same advance had answered at once `stopped: question`, France's opening question waiting, so it was tried again.)
- **A save whose player has no land**: `menu.load z1-no-country` answered after 13.2 s with session 2, 1445.2.20, `PRU`: at the game's first frame Prussia was the player. From the next request on, `session` was `not_in_game: no controllable player` and `menu` said `game`. So the answer a waiting load gets at a frame the player cannot play was not reached in the game: the game turned to observing after its first frame, not at it.
- **An answer larger than the pipe's buffer at a restart**: in the second process, observing there, the slow client asked for `screenshot --scale 25`; a second client's `events` read waited since the last event. Once the first bytes of the screenshot's answer came, `menu.return`. The restart came 0.12 s after them (`the game starts eu4.exe again`, 23:05:33.318); the waiting read was answered `not_ready` at once; the slow client read the screenshot's whole line, 1,273,442 bytes, by 23:05:35.776, 2.57 s after its first bytes, and its PNG (960x540, 954,980 bytes) decoded. The new process's core started at 23:05:35.404, 2.09 s after the restart began: the restart waited its 2 s for the answer still being read, and the old process, shutting down after it, kept writing until the client had it all. A first try in the first process sent the two requests mangled by the run's own quoting (both answered `bad_request`), and its return went ahead.
- **The end date**: in the fourth process, `menu.load z1-end-date` answered after 7.8 s: session 2, 1821.1.1, `FRA`. `time advance 30 --speed 1` answered after 4.3 s `stopped: paused` at 1821.1.3 ("the game was paused before the date, by the player, ..."), with the day events of 1821.1.2 and 1821.1.3; the end-of-game summary showed, 1444.11.11 to 1821.1.3. `time resume` and `time advance 1` were then both `rejected: the game is over: it is past its end date, 1821.1.2, and stays paused, as for the player, until the end-of-game summary is closed to play on`. Once the summary's continue button was clicked, `time resume` answered `null` and the game ran to 1821.1.4.
- **Costs**: in the logs read (the last three processes') every request answered at once took at most 0.1 ms; the first process's log, with `menu.saves`, was replaced by the later ones' before it was read.
- **Not seen in the running game**: a load or start given up after 30 s with the start accepted, whose flag giving up now clears (no start the game accepted and then took no step on came about, in this run or before); a load failing after the single player setup chose the save, which now says the save is still read; a waiting load or start answered at a frame the player cannot play (above; unit test); whether the build before would have cut the screenshot's answer off at the restart, since it was not run; the camera refusing while locked, after its arguments (nothing locked it); a screenshot given up as its thread starts; `FakeGame`'s new refusals and camera, the order of what a frame heard, the shared pipe name, calendar, thousandths and checks (unit tests).
- **Cleanup**: the game was closed by ending its process once the checks were done; no crash folder was made. The two test saves were deleted, and `settings.txt` and `pdx_login_cache.txt`, which the game rewrote, put back; both save folders, the framework's logs and settings and every file at the top of the user folder compared equal to the backup. The framework was uninstalled, which put back the earlier `version.dll`; the backups, logs and screenshots of the run were deleted.
- **Rebased onto C8, not run in the game**: the branch was then rebased onto the merge of C8 (diplomacy), keeping both sides: C8's `objects::diplomacy` and its `FakeGame` fields beside Z1's, the restart's doc naming C8's `pending` answer, and C8's poll test given the frame the player cannot play. That build passed the build, the tests and the offline checks against `eu4.exe`, and was never started in the game.

## 2026-10-30 Play-through (remote control Z2, tested by me)

Two short sessions of the game, played through `eu4-harmony game` alone, run from `eu4-harmony\eu4-harmony.exe` in the game folder, where `dev-install.ps1` now installs it with the rest of the player's files: nothing else drove the game, no click and no key, and `eu4-harmony game help` was read first, as an AI would. The first session's build was this branch at `Install and pack eu4-harmony.exe with the framework`, the second's at `Say in the game command's help what each error means and how a game goes`, with what the first session led to; both on the requirement branch at Z1's merge, 567 dependency points matching, 104 hooks and 4 writes, after the build, the tests and the offline checks passed. `eu4.exe` was started directly. Chinese playset with the unchanged regional-combat extension, system fonts. Before each session the files AGENTS.md lists were backed up, with every other file at the top of the user folder and the framework's whole folder. The sessions ran 8 and 4 minutes, each closed once its checks were done; nothing grew slow: a one-day advance at speed 5 took 0.10 to 0.16 s in the engine (0.25 to 0.31 s through the command line), ten days 0.5 to 2.9 s, and no request answered at once took more than 25 ms (a recruit refused; `menu.saves` 15 to 18 ms).

- **The main menu**: at startup `menu` answered after 41 s, `starting`, and `main_menu` 5 s later. `menu.saves` listed 139 saves, 25 with `missing_mods`, as in Z1's run. A test save, `z2-binary-missing-mod` (an ironman save with one mod's descriptor and name changed in its binary `meta`), was listed with that mod missing and refused by `menu.load`, `rejected` at once; the 92 ironman saves were listed with their players and dates and no missing mod.
- **A new game**: `menu.start FRA` answered after 9.6 s with session 1, 1444.11.11. The start-of-game introduction window stayed on screen in the screenshots until time ran. France's opening question (`flavor_fra.206`) was listed and answered; `time.advance 1` at speed 1 took 2.5 s, as speed 1 runs.
- **Observing**: `country.get FRA` (allied to Provence, five subjects), `province.get 183` (巴黎, infantry and cavalry allowed, ships not), `province.list` (4,941 provinces; France 20, England 40), `province.neighbors`, `army.list` (two armies and two fleets, the fleets with `province` null), `army.can_enter`, `army.in_province`, `events`.
- **Orders while paused**, the first session's main finding: after every `time.advance` the game is paused, and `army.find_route`, `army.move` and `army.stop` were refused `not_ready`, "game is paused". The only way on was `time.resume`, the order and `time.pause`, during which the game, at speed 5, ran on 11 days in 0.85 s. With the second session's build, at 1444.11.11 with the game paused, `army.find_route` answered `[4388]`, `army.move` and `army.stop` `null`, three `army_ordered` events came, the date stayed 1444.11.11, and the army reached 185 as soon as time ran.
- **Recruiting**: infantry in Paris answered `null`, and the next read had 10 ducats and 1,000 men less. In 1475, artillery was refused "the player has no unit type of the kind artillery" and a heavy ship "建立需要51¤。".
- **Questions and offers**: two questions answered (`flavor_fra.206`, `consort_events.51`), one in 1475 with two options (`incidents_bur_inheritance.301`); royal marriage offers from Scotland accepted and from Castile declined, both gone from `offer.list` at once. A question that came up on the last day of an advance was answered `date`; a later advance answered `question` at once.
- **Diplomacy**, first session: an alliance proposed to Scotland `accepted` (57 to 70 ms after it ran), one to Castile the same day refused "你必须等待一天才可以提出另一个结盟提议。" and `accepted` the next day; on 1444.12.12 `diplomacy.war_goals ENG` listed seven `cb_core`/`take_core` war goals, one claimed for Armagnac (`@AMG 收回核心省份拉布尔`), then `none`; `declare_war ENG cb_core --province 167` answered `declared` with "法兰西收复科之战" and `called: []`, France's three allies passed over with nothing said; the war had France and its subjects attacking, England, Leinster and Portugal defending. A white peace was refused during the diplomat's wait and later `declined` (74 ms).
- **Diplomacy**, second session: Castile refused an alliance with its reasons (rival; opinion -78); the declaration on England answered `passed_over` for Scotland and Provence, each with the call box's tooltip ("估计他们不会接受。", for 50 and against -1004, "苏格兰没有欠法兰西至少10点人情：-1000"). In the 1475 save, after the game console's `favors` with each ally, `diplomacy.war_goals HAB` offered `cb_humiliate_rotw`/`humiliate_rotw` with `target` HAB; declaring it with `--call-allies BOH,PAP` was `rejected` with the Papal State's tooltip and nothing sent (`country.get` had no war); with every ally, `declared` "法兰西羞辱奥地利之战", `called` Bohemia, `passed_over` the Ottomans ("正在打另一场战争：-1000") and the Papal State; two days later Bohemia and its subjects were among the attackers. - **Frames the player cannot play**: in the second session `time.advance 30 --speed 1` was under way when a second client ran `observe`; the advance answered `not_in_game`, "the player can no longer play the game (no controllable player)", 1.41 s after `observe` ran (Z1's run, before the 1 s wait: 0.75 s), and `session` then `not_in_game`, `menu` `game`.

After each, the save games folder and the framework's folder were replaced by the backup's and every file at the top of the user folder put back, which removed the test saves and whatever the game had rewritten; all 162 files compared equal to the backup. The framework was uninstalled, which put back the earlier `version.dll`; the backups, logs and screenshots of the run were deleted.

## 2026-10-30 Subject types in the country snapshot (tested by me)

## 2026-10-30 System fonts at the window's density (tested by me)

The game ran at 3840x2160 with the interface scale at 1.5 (`game_ui_scale` in `settings.txt`), where the player found Microsoft YaHei badly drawn. Four runs of `eu4.exe`, started directly, Chinese playset with the unchanged regional-combat extension; each went to the main menu, then `menu.start MNG` into a new game as Ming, never unpaused, with a screenshot of each through `eu4-harmony game screenshot`. Framework settings for the first three: system fonts, Microsoft YaHei for text, STLiti for the map; for the last, the player's own (bitmap fonts). The first run was the framework build installed before (for comparison), the other three this branch's, after the build, the tests and the offline checks against `eu4.exe` passed. Backups as AGENTS.md says, with the framework's whole folder and its installed files in the game folder.

- **Startup**: 579 dependency points match; 106 hooks, 4 writes.
- **The pages**: five of Microsoft YaHei, at 13.6 to 27.3 px, "1.50 px a GUI unit"; the map's STLiti at 1.00. In the second run they were 1024 pixels square, like the first's; from the third on 1536, each adding 9 MB of private bytes as it was created instead of 4. With bitmap fonts the six grown DXT5 pages were as before, at 1.00.
- **Before**: every system glyph was the page's pixels stretched 1.5 times by the linear filter: soft, its strokes uneven in weight. That was the whole of what the player saw; the glyphs themselves were anti-aliased, across only.
- **After**: the start-of-game introduction, the window's title, its tabs and its Close button, the outliner, the top bar's numbers, the main menu's buttons, the continue box and the version line are all sharp, at the size and place they had. The tabs, the Close button and the main menu's buttons are drawn through their own texture, now made 1.5 times as large: not cut off, not moved.
- **Line breaks**: the introduction's first line holds two characters more ("纷争"): a glyph's advance is now rounded to page pixels where it was rounded to GUI units, and the characters of that text came out about a pixel narrower each.
- **Bitmap fonts**: the introduction, the tabs and the outliner looked as with the build before; not compared pixel by pixel.
- **Not seen in the running game**: tooltips, event windows, the lobby and edit boxes with the change; the interface scale changed while the game runs (pages keep the density they loaded with); a scale of 1 or below (density 1, so nothing changes; not run); floating text; a page filling up; the game wanting textures in powers of two (unit test only); whether horizontal strokes look softer than with anti-aliasing across only at the sizes YaHei's own table hints (20 px here), which was judged from a rendering outside the game.
- **Cleanup**: each game was closed by ending its process, which left `graceful_exit=no` in `settings.txt`; that file, `pdx_login_cache.txt` (rewritten by the game) and the framework's logs were put back from the backup, and every backed-up file compared equal to it; no save was written and no crash folder made. The framework files installed before the run were put back in the game folder; the backups and screenshots of the run were deleted.

The window stayed where it had been dragged, now titled "（还有 3 条）". Closing it showed the first test notice in the middle, "（还有 2 条）".

The build before was not run with this extension.
- **The glyph table, now kept by font**: in the second process, with bitmap fonts, the log said "994216 font glyphs above 0xFF (0 duplicates), kept as 6 shared sets of 115053 in all", as the backed-up log of a bitmap run before the fixes did. The main menu was drawn in the bitmap fonts. With system fonts, in the first process, "0 shared sets of 0", as before.
- **The regional-combat extension's own tests**, in a copy of its repository built against the new SDK: all 16 pass with the stricter `FakeGame`.
- **Not seen in the running game**: input method text kept from the game while the settings window is open. Codex's computer use, asked to try it, was refused the game: the prompt that lets it use an app cannot be answered in `codex exec`. A font load or caret move that faults (nothing made one fault); the settings window (not opened); the SDK's route and table changes, and `FakeGame` (unit tests).

## 2026-10-30 Regional combat's radius box, straits, defeat and ironman saves (tested by me)

What the F1 run left unseen of the regional-combat extension. The build was master at `Draw the map font's outline around system glyphs on the map`, after the build, the tests and the offline checks against `eu4.exe` passed. The regional-combat extension was rebuilt for the first time since the SDK changes, from its repository's master (`Move texts to EU4 Harmony localisation files, with English`) against this SDK, in a copy of the repository; its 16 tests pass, and the DLL it made replaced the one in the mod for the run. `eu4.exe` was started directly, Chinese playset, system fonts. Backups as AGENTS.md says, with the framework files installed in the game folder and the mod's DLL copied aside. The game was driven by `eu4-harmony game` and `tools/game-input.ps1`.

- **Startup**: 579 dependency points match; 106 hooks, 4 writes; the extension loaded.
- **The radius box**: a new game as Byzantium, its army (8 regiments) selected in Constantinople. A right click on the extension's button opened the box at "半径 5" with the map preview; two clicks on its plus button showed "半径 7", and its apply button closed the box, put the map mode back and logged "army 111669149750 radius 7". Opened again it read "半径 7"; the minus button made it "半径 6", the cancel button closed it, and opened once more it read "半径 7".
- **Straits**: at 1444.12.11, war on the Ottomans (`cb_core`, Edirne; the game refuses every declaration before that date). `province.neighbors 151` lists 316 across a strait; the adjacency file has both the Bosporus (151–316) and the Dardanelles (4779–2296) through the Sea of Marmara (1320).
  - Open: `army.can_enter 316` turned from false to true with the war, and both straits read open. The radius box's preview at 7 took in Thrace, the Morea and much of Anatolia across the straits.
  - Blocked: on 1444.12.14, with nothing done, the Bosporus read closed to the Byzantine army for one day. To hold a block, the Byzantine fleet was sent into the Sea of Marmara (where the game said it blockaded Gallipoli, Kocaeli and two more ports), and `tag TUR` in the game console made the player the Ottomans. For their army, the Bosporus read closed and the Dardanelles open. The game's own route from Thessaly to Kocaeli agreed with that: `[148,4705,4779,2296,317,316]`, across the Dardanelles. Why the Dardanelles stayed open for the Ottomans was not looked into; their fleet lay in Gallipoli's port, at that strait's end. Their 19-ship fleet was then sent into the Sea of Marmara, and on 1444.12.25 `tag BYZ` made the player Byzantium again. Both straits read closed to its army, and the preview at 7 took in Thrace and the Morea but none of Anatolia.
- **Defeat**: after `army.stop` cleared the route the game had given the army while the player was the Ottomans, a left click turned its task on, "center 151 radius 7". On 1444.12.27 it ordered "move to Some(149): accepted". From 1444.12.29 the army fought in Constantinople, and from the next day the task logged "deferred: army cannot take orders now" with `in_battle: true` each day. On 1445.1.2 the army was gone (`army.get` answered `not_found`), remote control's events had `army_defeated` for it, and the extension logged "task off: lost a battle". Later, in the ironman save below, a task turned on for a Castilian army of three regiments also ended in "task off: lost a battle", with that army destroyed too.
- **Sessions on a change of country**: each `tag` in the game console started a new session ("session 2 started (from a loaded save: false)"), and the extension started it with no task ("restored 0 task(s)"). The radius applied before showed as 5 again, and the 7 had to be applied once more.
- **Ironman saves**: a copy of the ironman save `Castilla-5` as `rc-ironman-test` (Castile, 1470.3.13, on a crusade against the Ottomans), loaded with `menu.load`. The game rewrites an ironman save it loads: the file's digest changed as the session started.
  - Writing: the half-year autosave of 1470.7.1 and the save the game makes when it leaves for the main menu (`menu.return`) each wrote extension save data under the file's new digest ("saves: wrote extension data for …rc-ironman-test.eu4").
  - Restoring: a task turned on for the army in Constantinople (14 regiments, with followers, so the task gives it no order) with radius 8 was saved on leaving. Loading the save gave "loaded … with data for 1 extension(s)" and "session 2: restored 1 task(s)". Its radius box opened at "半径 8", and the preview was centered on 151.
  - **Lost after a crash (a defect)**: the save the game makes while loading writes no extension save data. The framework makes the loaded data current only at the session's first safe frame (`ext::frame`), after that save. The game was then ended before any other save, as a crash would end it; started again and loaded, it gave "data for 0 extension(s)" and "restored 0 task(s)", and the task was gone. The standalone regional combat before the port had handled this save.
- **Seen on the way**: as the Ottomans, a right click on the Sea of Marmara with their movement-locked army selected raised `army_ordered` for that army, though its route stayed `[149,151]`. Not looked into.
- **Not seen in the running game**: the extension ordering a move across a strait; a strait closing while a task's army was on its way across; a defeat the army survived (both armies were destroyed); a normal save's own rewrite on loading.
- **Cleanup**: each game was closed by ending its process; no crash folder was made. `player-state.ps1 -Restore` listed what the run changed (the autosaves, the test save and its `_Backup`, four extension save data files, the framework's logs, `continue_game.json`, `pdx_login_cache.txt`, `shortcuts.txt`), put each back and found every file as the backup had it. The framework files installed before the run and the mod's own DLL were put back and compared equal to their copies; the regional-combat repository has no change. The copy of the repository, its build, the backups, logs and screenshots of the run were deleted.

Afterwards the player, with this build installed, went through its two weight lists: Microsoft YaHei's three weights, a weight kept or stepped down as the family changed, Noto Serif SC's seven, saving and starting again. They reported no problem; what each step showed was not written down.

## 2026-10-31 Extension save data in the save the game makes while loading an ironman game (tested by me)

The defect the run of 2026-10-30 found: the save the game makes while loading an ironman game wrote no extension save data, so a crash before the next save lost it. The loader's observer now makes the loaded data current as soon as the load has replaced the world, not at the session's first safe frame. The build was that change on master at `Let the player choose the weight of the text font and the map font (#12)`, after the build, the tests and the offline checks against `eu4.exe` passed. The regional-combat extension was rebuilt from its repository's master against this SDK, in a copy of the repository (its 16 tests pass), and its DLL replaced the mod's for the run. `eu4.exe` was started directly, three times. Backups as AGENTS.md says, with the framework files installed in the game folder and the mod's DLL copied aside. The game was driven by `eu4-harmony game` alone.

- **Startup**: 579 dependency points match; 106 hooks, 4 writes; the extension loaded.
- **The save**: a copy of the ironman save `Castilla-5` as `rc-ironman-test` (Castile, 1470.3.13), loaded once ("data for 0 extension(s)"; the game rewrote the file) and the game ended. Extension save data for the file as it then was was written by hand, as the framework writes it: the file's digest, path and size, the date as the game counts it (56678904), and a task turned on with radius 8 for the army in Constantinople (`28136330756150`, 14 regiments). So no army had to be selected with clicks; the framework writing such a file itself was seen on 2026-10-30.
- **Loading**: "loaded … with data for 1 extension(s)", 4.4 s later "saves: wrote extension data for …rc-ironman-test.eu4" under the digest the file had after the load, with the same task, and 0.14 s after that "session 2 started (from a loaded save: true)" and the extension's "restored 1 task(s)". The order is the one the change relies on: the game's save comes after its loader returns and before the session's first safe frame.
- **After a crash**: the game was then ended at once, as a crash would end it, the save unchanged. Started again and loaded, it gave "data for 1 extension(s)", the save made while loading wrote the data again, and "restored 1 task(s)", the army still in 151.
- **Not seen in the running game**: a normal save or a new game with the change (neither is saved while loading; a unit test covers a load that does not replace the world leaving the data alone); a load whose save could not be identified; the radius box on the restored task.
- **Cleanup**: each game was closed by ending its process; no crash folder was made. `player-state.ps1 -Restore` listed what the run changed (the test save and its `_Backup`, three extension save data files, the framework's logs, `pdx_login_cache.txt`, `settings.txt`, `shortcuts.txt`), put each back and found every file as the backup had it. The framework files and the mod's DLL were put back and compared equal to their copies; the regional-combat repository has no change. The copy of the repository, its build and the backups were deleted.
