## 2026-11-05 Player actions A3: province administration and buildings (tested by me)

A3 of [player-actions.md](player-actions.md), a developer build of this branch with the command log (the A3 commands' types only), within a claim, started hidden; the player's playset. Three processes, each a new game as Castile at 1444.11.11, driven through remote control; the second played to 1449.8.1, the game's questions answered with their first option. The console's `cash`, `adm`, `dip` and `own 223` gave the player money, points and Granada where an action needed them. What was found in the game's code is in [research/province-view.md](research/province-view.md).

- **`province.options`** gave every action of the province view for Madrid (217), Pontevedra (4555) and Granada (223, Granada's, then the player's), each with the game's reasons in the player language as the buttons' tooltips write them, and the prices: developing 54 points, then 55; coring 70 to 150 adm; changing culture 37 to 140 dip; 30 edicts, those of other ages or nations refused with their conditions. A foreign province got the game's own refusals for every action ("这个省份不属于你或你属国…", "只能将属于我们的省份转换为核心省份。", the four reasons for a culture change).
- **Developing**: `develop_manpower 217` raised Madrid's base manpower from 5 to 6 and the next was refused, "你需要55 军事点数才能在马德里执行提高基础人力。"; with points, `develop_tax` and `develop_production` raised both to 6. The first try was refused by the command layer: the power spending's `second` does not come back from the game's reader; without it, the form is the one the AI's commands have.
- **Exploiting**: `exploit_tax 4555` took Pontevedra's base tax from 3 to 2 and gave 12.7 ducats; then every exploit there was refused until 1464.11.11 with the game's text. `exploit_production` in Madrid, which has no port, was refused with "这里没有水手。".
- **Autonomy**: `raise_autonomy 4555` was done, then both buttons were refused until 1474.11.11. `lower_autonomy` was refused below 10% and, in Granada while it was being cored, with "由于自治度下限为90.00%…"; once Granada was a core (1448), `lower_autonomy 223` was done and refused again until 1478.10.25.
- **States**: `make_territory 4555` made Galicia a territory and `make_state 4555` a state again, each refused the other way round with the game's two lines (the reason and the button's own hint, "（点击设置为直属州）"). `make_territory 217` made Southern Castile a territory, and `make_state 217` a state again.
- **Edicts**: `set_edict 217 edict_advancement_effort` set it, and changing or cancelling it was refused for a year, "1445.11.11之前促进进步不可以被改变或者移除。"; `edict_of_absolutism` was refused with its two conditions; an unknown key was `invalid_argument`. In 1448.10 `cancel_edict 4555` cancelled Galicia's edict, after which it was refused with "当地没有法令可供废除！".
- **Coring**: `core 223` spent 70 adm and started coring Granada, until 1446.5.11; `options` then offered `cancel_core`, which stopped it, and `core` again started it.
- **Culture**: `change_culture 4555` with too few points was refused with "你需要80dip以转换该省份的文化，但你目前仅有42。" (the culture button's own check, which the game's culture check leaves out); with points, it started converting Pontevedra to Castilian, until 1450.9.11, and `cancel_culture 4555` stopped it. `change_culture 217`, Castilian already, was refused by the framework.
- **Buildings**: `building.options 217` listed every building with its price and the game's reasons ("集市需要外交科技达到4级。", "这个工场与马德里出产的产品不适配。"); `build 217 fort_15th` started a fort, to be finished 1447.3.11, after which every build there was refused with "城堡已正在马德里建造"; `cancel 217` cancelled it; `destroy 219 fort_15th` destroyed the fort of province 219, the only building of Castile's at the start; a building that does not stand there, and another country's province, were refused by the framework.
- **The building finished event**: read by remote control with `--kinds building_finished`, four buildings of other countries were heard by 1448.10 (natives' fields, a temple), and with `--countries CAS` Madrid's fort, built again in 1446.10, at 1449.2.1. The first try ended the game through the framework's own fatal error: the observer used the construction's class, which no dependency point checked; a point now keeps the constructor's instruction that sets it.
- **Not tried**: an extension's `on_building_finished` (only remote control read the event); cancelling a building another country is building in the player's province; destroying under siege; making the capital's area a territory (the command's check refuses it, the tooltip does not); a refusal by a command's own check after the framework's checks passed; the player's view against the full view, which these reads do not differ in.
- **Cleanup**: the release put the player's files back (every file as the backup had it) and the game folder's framework files.

## 2026-11-05 Player actions A2: monarch points and the treasury (tested by me)

A2 of [player-actions.md](player-actions.md), a developer build with the command log, within a claim, started hidden; the player's playset, the game in Chinese. Two new games as France at 1444.11.11, driven through remote control; the panels compared on screenshots.

- **Technology**: `technology.options` gave levels 3, 3, 3 at 598 points each, each refused with the view's lines ("没有足够的军事点数研究这一级科技！你需要 598 点而现在只有 84 点可用。", "我们预计将在1452年2月拥有足够点数来完成它。"). The first build gave only the second line; the view's tooltip has both, and the second build gives both. After the console's `powerpoints 1000`, `technology.research mil` took 598 military points and the view showed military 4; the next level cost 1231 ("领先于时代 +90%" in the view), its tooltip reading "你需要 1231 点而现在只有 486 点可用" as the refusal did.
- **National focus**: France starts on diplomatic; `focus.options` said so and offered cancelling, as the court view's tooltip ("点击将您的国家焦点从外交点数移除"). `focus.choose dip` was refused ("the national focus is on dip already"); `focus.choose adm` set it (the view's administrative gain went from 6 to 9); then `focus.cancel` and `focus.choose mil` were refused with the game's line, "每20年只能改变一次你的国家焦点，1464年11月11日后才能再次改变国家焦点。", the same as the court view's tooltip.
- **Stability**: at 84 administrative points refused with "你没有足够的adm提高稳定度！你需要100而可用点数只有84。"; with the points, three raises took stability from 0 to 3, the fourth was refused, "你的稳定度已达到最高。".
- **Economy**: `take_loan` offered 128 ducats, as the economy view's button; taken twice, the treasury grew by 256. `reduce_inflation` at 0 inflation was refused with the view's tooltip ("你目前通货膨胀不够高，你只能在它高于0时才能手动降低。", base cost 75, 74 with innovativeness, as `economy.options` said); after the loans it reduced inflation once and was refused again. `raise_mercantilism` was refused at 98 diplomatic points ("你需要100dip来提高重商主义，而你仅有98。"), then raised mercantilism from 10% to 11% for 99 points, the trade view's tooltip saying 99.
- **Loans**: `loan.list` gave each bank loan, 128 ducats at 4%, due 1449.11.11, repaying it 153.572, as the loan window shows them ("128 及 4.00%的利息。（总计：153.57）"). The first build read every loan's ID as the same (the ID's type, not its number); the second gives 1, 2, 3…. `loan.repay 1` paid 153.572 and the loan was gone; again, `not_found`. With the treasury at −235 (console `cash -4500`) every repay was refused with "你无力支付153.57¤以偿还贷款！", the loan window's tooltip on its disabled button.
- **Commands**: the command log showed the framework's commands run as the game writes them, `take_bank_loan={ … country="FRA" interest_modifier=0.000 duration=-1 fixed_interest=no interest=0.000 estate_loan=no }`, the keys left out at the game's defaults. The buttons were not pressed by hand to compare their commands.
- **Not tried**: too many bank loans (34 were still allowed), the rule against ruining a nation, the scripted functions' conditions (empty in the unmodded game), `may_not_reduce_inflation`, mercantilism at its most, a technology at its last level, loans from countries or estates, an extension calling these (only remote control was used).
- **Cleanup**: the release put the player's files back and the game folder's framework files; the screenshots were deleted.

## 2026-11-05 Player actions A4: armies and fleets (tested by me)

A4 of [player-actions.md](player-actions.md), a developer build with the command log of this branch, within a claim, started hidden; the player's playset (a Chinese localisation mod among it). Three new games as England at 1444.11.11, driven through remote control only: the first to 1444.12.2 (it ended at the first `siege.options`, below), the second to 1445.11.26, the third only for the leaders' reasons.

- **The first `siege.options` ended the game**, by the framework's own guard: "the framework used 0x1c55480, which no dependency point checks", the spending's vtable the framework puts in the spending it asks the cost of. A dependency point now checks it (the siege view's button that makes the same spending), and the run went on in a new game.
- **Leaders**: `leader.options` gave the general and the conquistador "你需要至少50mil。" with 0 military points (the number was missing until the text was read unformatted, `game_string::unformatted`: the game's lookup without values leaves `$COST$` out), the admiral `can` at 50 dip, the conquistador and the explorer "你需要「探索新世界」国家理念才能招募探险家和征服者。", the conquistador the points as well. `leader.recruit admiral` recruited "安东尼 德 克利福德" (a name the game picked) and took 50 dip; `general` and `explorer` were refused with those reasons. `leader.assign` gave John Talbot (175) to the army in London and the admiral to a fleet, which `leader.list` then showed as their `army`; the general to a fleet was refused by the framework; `leader.dismiss` of a leader leading a unit was refused; `leader.remove` took both off, and `leader.dismiss 4209` then removed the admiral from the list.
- **Mercenaries**: `mercenary.list` listed the companies with their content keys (`merc_routiers`, `merc_gallowglasses`, …), names, regiments, cost (115.056 ducats for 10 regiments) and upkeep, each refused "我们现在承担不起115¤的费用。" with 71 ducats; with 500 more, `mercenary.hire merc_routiers 236` took 115 ducats and the list then said "大路佣兵已经被我们雇佣了！"; in Paris it was refused "英格兰不是巴黎的控制者。"; an unknown key was not_found. In the second game `merc_gallowglasses` hired in Cumbria stood there as an army of 8 regiments within 40 days.
- **Forts**: `fort.list` listed England's six forts with their levels, all `can`; `fort.mothball 246` mothballed Northumberland's (the list then said `mothballed`), `fort.reopen` reopened it; reopening an open fort, mothballing a mothballed one and a province without a fort of the player's were refused by the framework.
- **The army panel**: `army.options` for London's army: split and disband `can`, merge "仅当选中两个单位时才能进行重组。", consolidate "所有的军队都已满员。", drill "你不能在军队没有领袖时进行操练。" (the bullet the game starts each line with is left out since); after a battle in Scotland, consolidate `can`, and `army.consolidate` took the army from 14 regiments to 12; disband in occupied Dumfries "不能在被敌人占领的领土或者临近敌人的军队时解散单位。". `army.split` split 14 regiments into 7 and 7 and answered the new army; `army.merge` of the two made one of 14; `army.disband` removed a split one; `army.drill` with a leader started drilling (options then offered `stop_drill`), `army.stop_drill` stopped it, and each a second time was refused. Fleets in a naval battle: merge "仅当选中的单位都未处于战斗状态时才能进行重组。", disband "此单位在战斗中。"; after it, `army.merge` of two fleets made one of 32 ships and `army.split` two of 16.
- **Fleets moving**: `army.route_to` for a fleet gave `[1270,1271,1272,1274,1275,1276]`; `army.move` set the route, the fleets sailed from London to the Irish Sea (1284) within a month, and `army.get` showed their sea zone and ships. Missions: after a move, `army.options` offered patrol and evade, and `army.mission … patrol` and `protect_trade --target 5` were taken; evade after `army.stop_mission` (which leaves no route) was refused by the game's command without a reason; `blockade` and `hunt_fleets` with the region numbers 0, 5 and 40 were taken by the command. What a mission does once taken was not read back: the framework reads no mission.
- **Transport**: `army.move <army> 87 --transports` (Calais, across the Channel; `army.route_to` found no route there) set the army busy with a route to the coast, and 20 days later it stood in Calais. **Not verified: boarding by `army.move` onto a sea zone with a fleet of the player's, landing from it, armies moving aboard**: the route query found no route from London, nor from Dumfries, to the sea zone where the player's fleet was, and seven transports ordered in Gwynedd were never built (the orders took no money); why was not worked out.
- **Sieges**: England at war with Scotland (France and its allies joined). Besieging Roxburghshire (no fort): the three refused "该地无要塞，没有城墙可轰击。" and "该地无要塞，无可供强攻的目标。"; a province without a siege was refused by the framework. Besieging Dumfries (fort level 2): bombard "你需要数量超过要塞等级的满员炮兵团，……你目前只有0支炮兵团，但轰击此要塞需要2支。", naval bombard "你在相邻的海域没有舰队……" with the cannons' line, assault "你不可以在城墙破裂之前强攻这座要塞。"; with the fleets next to it, naval bombard "需要50mil来进行海上炮击，而你只有40。"; with 200 more, `siege.naval_bombard` took 50 mil and made a breach (bombard and naval bombard then "这个要塞已经出现城墙缺口。", assault `can` at 5 mil), `siege.assault` took 5 mil (then "你正在强攻，不能打出城墙缺口。" and "现在我们已经开始强攻此城了。"), and Dumfries fell to the assault within 10 days. **Not verified: an artillery barrage carried out** (England had no artillery).
- **The battle event**: after a read of `--kinds battle`, two naval battles of England's fleets were heard, such as `{"province":1285,"naval":true,"attackers":["ENG"],"defenders":["ULS"],"winner":"attackers","attacker_losses":0,"defender_losses":2000}` (the losses then in thousandths, 2 ships; divided since), and a land battle in Roxburghshire, England's army of 14 regiments losing to Lorraine, Ulster and Scotland, `attacker_losses` 12,000,000 (12,000 men in thousandths), heard once, after `army_defeated` for the same army. Read with `--countries FRA`, none passed. **Not verified**: a battle the player only sees (none in sight was looked for), the full view's every battle, an extension's `on_battle`, and what the event costs the game (not measured into performance.md).
- **Not tried**: an extension calling any of these; recruiting a conquistador or an explorer the player can recruit; the fort box's own reasons (under siege, not the controller); mothballing fleets; merging units of different provinces.
## 2026-11-05 Player actions A5: war and peace (tested by me)

A5 of [player-actions.md](player-actions.md), a player build of this branch, within a claim, started hidden; the player's playset. Two processes, each a new game as France at 1444.11.11, driven through remote control only; the war set up with `diplomacy.declare_war` and the game console's `winwars`, `control`, `favors`.

- **Events**: after a read of `--kinds war_declared,peace_made,province_owner_changed`, the days to 1445.2.12 gave `war_declared` for Timurids on Qom ("阿贾姆叛乱") and Sirhind on Delhi, and `province_owner_changed` 59 from Saxony to Thuringia. France's declaration on England gave `war_declared` FRA on ENG "法兰西-英格兰战争"; read with `--countries FRA` it alone came, with `--countries ENG` the declaration, the peace and the province. The peace with England gave `peace_made` with the attackers France and its five appanages and the defenders England, Leinster, Ulster and Portugal, `ended` true, then `province_owner_changed` 174 ENG to FRA; the offered peace with Brittany `peace_made` and 4111 FRA to BRI. Extensions' handlers were not tried.
- **`peace.terms ENG`** listed 67 terms: 40 `demand_provinces` (Calais 87 at 11, Bordeaux 174 at 18, ...), `return_cores` to Armagnac, Provence and Scotland, `release_annexed` (Gascony 28, Normandy 33, ...), `annul_treaties` with Leinster, Ulster and Portugal, `end_rivalry`, `war_reparations` 10, `concede_defeat` 10, `steer_trade` 60, and `gold` with at most 860 ducats; `become_vassal` cost 357 and was refused "不能提出超过100%战争分数价值的条件", `pillage_capital_state` "伦敦未被法兰西或它的一个属国占领。" with the term's description as its second line, as the tooltip writes it. Names of a few terms keep the game's `@ENG` flag codes ("@ENG法兰西的附庸"). With `--offer` against Brittany: Brittany's demands of France, most refused "不能选择这个省份，因为布列塔尼无法将其核心化。". Against Castile, not at war: `rejected` "the player is not at war with CAS". Not compared with the peace view on screen.
- **`peace.evaluate`**: Calais alone cost 12 (11 in the list) and England would not accept: −1000 "在未占领与加莱陆路相连的要塞的情况下索取该省份：", −1000 "至少需要10点战争分数来提出要求：", −32 "要求超出战争分数：" among seven factors, total −2093; while the diplomat was away, `can` false with "最近已经派遣过外交官，所以直到1445年3月13日我们才能再次派遣。". Refusals: `concede_defeat` with another term "要求承认战败时，不能附带其他任何条件。"; `humiliate`, not offered, `invalid_argument`; a term twice `invalid_argument`; 2000 ducats "from 0 to 860.0 ducats". After `winwars`: Bordeaux, war reparations and annulling the treaty with Portugal cost 38, accepted, the one factor "被彻底击败：" +100.
- **Gold**: in the first process gold had no effect (cost 0, the answer unchanged): the game's setter found no chosen gold term. With the fix (the term's effect taken off and put on again around the amount) 100, 300 and 860 ducats cost 3, 9 and 25. Demanding 300 ducats and Calais was accepted; France's treasury went from 74.1 to 302.8, 228.7 more, not 300: why was not found.
- **`peace.demand`, `peace.offer`**: the demand to England `accepted` and the war ended; Bordeaux was France's. Offering Saintonge (4111) to Brittany, refused for the diplomat first, then `accepted` a month later.
- **`diplomacy.call_to_arms`**: Provence, France's ally, before any war: `rejected` "the diplomacy panel offers no call to arms to PRO"; in the war on England `rejected` "它们不会接受。" (the panel's check, the AI would decline), the same after the console's `favors PRO 50`; in the second process, at war with Brittany, the call was sent and Provence `declined`. Burgundy, no ally: no call offered. Scotland, allied the same day, joined Brittany's side as Brittany's ally and was offered no call. No ally that accepted was seen.
- **`occupation`**: Calais, England's, controlled by England: `can` false "只有控制者能将省份控制权转移给其他国家。"; after `control 87 FRA`, the allies Orléans, Auvergne, Foix, Bourbon and Armagnac (France's appanages; Provence was not in the war); to Provence `rejected` "the transfer occupation window does not list PRO to take province 87"; to Orléans `{"controller": "ORL"}`, and then, Orléans holding it, France could still hand it on (`FRA` among the allies listed) and gave it to Bourbon. Not compared with the window on screen.
- **Not tried**: an ally accepting a call to arms; a scripted peace treaty term, a colonial region and the terms France was not offered (`humiliate`, `revoke_reform`, religion and government); a peace offer to an ally of the war's leader (a separate peace); extensions' calls and handlers; the peace view on screen.
- **Cleanup**: the release put the player's files back (every file as the backup had it) and the game folder's framework files.
## 2026-11-05 Player actions A7: government (tested by me)

A7 of [player-actions.md](player-actions.md), a developer build of this branch (its command log on), within two claims, started hidden. First, the AI's own commands logged for five game years from the save `Castilla-4` (the Netherlands, 1592.4.5) at speed 5, which gave the written forms of the A7 commands ([research/government.md](research/government.md)). Then the same save loaded again for each build, the player the Netherlands, driven through remote control.

- **Reads**: `idea.list` gave the four groups taken (all ideas bought) first and 22 others, each refused with "the player has no free idea group slot"; `policy.list` four policies, `vassal_integration_act` enabled and refused with "－你已经启用了这一政策。"; `government.options` rank 3, reform progress 97.075, the reforms adopted, and raising the rank refused with "－你的政府等级已达最高！"; `government.reforms` 11 tiers, nine reached, changing a reform there costing 50, the next tier costing 420; `estate.list` the clergy, nobles and burghers with loyalty, influence and land share and their privileges, `estate_burghers_admirals` refused with "海军传统至少为30"; `estate.interactions` `seize_land` refused with "可在1594.03.09再次使用"; `parliament.options` no parliament; `mission.list` 64 missions in five columns, the uncompleted ones with their conditions ("拥有至少50艘轻型战舰"); `decision.list` seven, two takeable.
- **Actions**: enabling `the_royal_commission_act` enabled it (listed enabled after); disabling `vassal_integration_act` disabled it, and disabling the policy just enabled was refused by the game's command with no reason; changing tier 0's reform to `autocracy_reform` replaced `austrian_dual_monarchy` and took 50 reform progress; granting and revoking `estate_nobles_levies` both went through (the nobles' loyalty 10 lower after); `sale_of_titles` went through (every estate's land share higher, the clergy's and burghers' loyalty 10 higher) and was then refused with its cooldown; `development_of_amsterdam` was taken and left the list; abandoning `spy_ideas`, taking `aristocracy_ideas` and buying `serfdom` for 320 military points went through (571 to 251 points).
- **Refusals**: an enabled policy, an unknown policy (`not_found`), a group with no slot free, a group with every idea bought or not taken, the rank at its highest, a reform with too little progress or adopted already, a privilege not granted, a decision with its failing conditions (all seven lines of `ned_unite_two_crowns`), an unknown decision and mission, a completed mission, a mission with its conditions, a second idea with too few points ("buying it takes 320 mil points, and the player has 251"), debating and a seat without a parliament.
- **Found by trying**: the first build ended the game at `government.reforms` with "the framework used 0x79ead0, which no dependency point checks": four functions called had no point of their own; added. A change command naming the reform's place in the stack (1) replaced the second tier's reform instead: the member is the tier's index. The missions view's columns hold mission series, not missions (the first build faulted in writing the conditions of a series as a mission's). An idea's key is at `+0x158`. The database's group `none` was listed; it is not now.
- **Not tried**: parliament with a player who has one; a mission the player can complete; raising the rank where the game allows it; changing a reform for free; any panel compared on screen, the estates' influence among them; an extension calling the capabilities.
- **Cleanup**: both releases put the player's files back (every file as the backup had it) and the game folder's framework files.
## 2026-11-05 Player actions A8: missionaries, colonists, trade companies, centers of trade, merchants (tested by me)

A8 of [player-actions.md](player-actions.md), developer builds of this branch with the command log (and, for the native policy, the test switches' posts), within a claim, started hidden; the player's playset, a 3840x2160 window. A new game as the Ottomans at 1444.11.11, then `tag POR` from the game console; then new games as Portugal and its autosave loaded again after each build. Everything through remote control; the console's `add_colonist`, `own`, `cash` and `tag` set the cases up. Each command the layer posted showed in the command log as the game executed it, in the written form [research/envoys.md](research/envoys.md) gives.

- **Missionaries** (the Ottomans, one missionary): `missionary.options` allowed Edirne (149) and other Orthodox provinces, each at its own upkeep (0.584 ducats a month for Edirne), refused Anatolia's with "这些民众已经信奉了正统信仰。" and Constantinople (151, Byzantium's) with "你不能将传教士派往你尚未控制的省份。". `missionary.send 149` was executed (`send_missionary={ … country="TUR" owned_by="TUR" location=149 }`); then 149 read `working` with progress 0 and its send refused with "一名传教士已在埃迪尔内工作。 于1444.11.11完成。"; a second send elsewhere was refused with "没有可供派遣的传教士。"; `missionary.recall 149` posted `cancel=yes` and 149 was free again; a second recall was refused by the framework ("the player has no missionary there"); province 99999 was `not_found`. `missionary.automatic` read 1 missionary, 0 automatic; raise made it 1, a second raise and a second lower were refused by the framework, lower made it 0.
- **Centers of trade** (Portugal, Lisbon 227 at level 2, 38 ducats): upgrade refused with "升级到世界级港口需要25.00发展度，但是该省份仅有16.00" and "升级此贸易中心需要1000.00¤，而你只有38.10¤。"; downgrade (10 prestige) was executed: level 1, prestige 4.963 → -5.037; a second downgrade refused with "无法降级此贸易中心，已经是最低级别！" and "负威望时无法降级贸易中心！"; after `cash 2000` the upgrade (200 ducats) was executed, level 2, treasury down by 200. Stockholm (1, Sweden's) was refused with the game's "所有者变更时贸易中心将被降一级。" among its reasons; a province without one was refused by the framework.
- **Merchants** (Portugal, two merchants steering in Tunis and Safi): `merchant.list` and `merchant.options` gave the missions and the nodes downstream; `steer_to tunis valencia` was executed and Tunis then steered to Valencia; steering in the home node Sevilla was refused with "你不能在你获取贸易收入的节点引导贸易。" and a steer there with "你在本埠节点永远只能获取贸易收入，你无法转移这里的贸易价值。"; `recall tunis` was executed and the merchant left the list; sending with none free was refused with "必须拥有一个可用商人。"; `steer genua` was executed and read `arriving` until it got there; `collect genua` while it travelled changed its mission (`type=0` for the same merchant, as the game's own button does); recalling it on its way was refused by the framework. A first build counted a merchant on its way as not there, and a recalled one as still going; both were fixed (the mission's node `+0x80` and phase `+0x98`) and tried again.
- **Colonists** (Portugal, after `add_colonist`): every province was refused with "在派遣殖民队之前，我们需要选择原住民政策。" until a native policy was set (a `set_native_policy` posted by the test switches, which the game's check takes only once the country has a colonist, so the game was saved and loaded); then the Azores (367) and Madeira (368), not yet discovered, were refused with no reason (the panel shows nothing there), and Arguin (1111) was allowed. `colonist.send 1111` was executed; 1111 then read `working`, its send refused with "一支殖民队将在1445年1月04日到达。其成功率为1.40%。" and "殖民者正从葡萄牙赶来。"; with none left another send was refused with "没有可供派遣的殖民队。". `colonist.recall` was executed, but the colonist stays until the game's next day, then the province is free again.
- **Trade companies** (Portugal, Goa 2030 given with `own`): `trade_company.add 2030` was executed and Goa read `in_trade_company`; a second add was refused with no reason (the panel shows the remove button then); Lisbon was refused with "贸易公司只能在自治领地上建立。". The investments of Goa's area listed 15 with their names in the player language ("葡萄牙定居点"), each refused for want of money with "你负担不起200¤" and the like; with money, `trade_company.invest 2030 local_quarter` took 200 ducats and the investment then read `made`, refused again with "你已经在卡纳达区域投资过当地驻防了。"; an unknown key was `not_found`; `trade_company.remove 2030` was executed, and adding again was refused with "1449年12月07日前不能将这个省份添加给贸易公司。". A first build read the investments' array as a vector (it is a pointer and a count) and kept the condition lines that hold and their heading; both were fixed and tried again.
- **Not tried**: the panels on screen (only the reasons' texts were compared with what the game writes for its tooltips); a colony growing to a city; a missionary's conversion finishing; a merchant collecting outside its home node with a trade post; an extension calling these capabilities; a subject's center of trade; a save other than the run's own autosave.
- **Cleanup**: the release put the player's files back (every file as the backup had it, the run's autosaves among them) and the game folder's framework files; `dev.toml` and the screenshots were deleted.

## 2026-11-05 Player actions A6: the diplomacy panel, subjects and rivals (tested by me)

A developer build of this branch with the command log (`dev-command-log`, logging `diplomaticactioncommand`, `subject`, `add_rival` and `remove_rival`), installed within a claim and started hidden; new games as France at 1444.11.11, driven through `eu4-harmony game …`; the player's Chinese playset, 3840x2160. Three processes: the first ended at the first subject request (below), the second and third ran the rest.

- **`diplomacy.options CAS`** listed 56 actions in the panel's order, each named by its capability, with its button text as the game's (宣战, 提议结盟, 建立包围网, …) and the game's reasons for each that could not be sent (宣战: "在1444年12月11日之前不能宣战。"; 要求附庸: two reasons at once; the spy actions: the network's size and the technology). The diplomacy panel opened on Castile from its province showed the same: 提议结盟 green with its tick, 建立包围网 greyed, 提供遣外雇佣军团 enabled. **Acceptance**: `propose_alliance` had `accepts` true, total 60, and five factors (陆军实力 -1, 海军实力 -5, 好感度 +6, 态度是友好 +50, 外交声誉 +10); the tick's tooltip said 他们很可能会接受, 赞成 66, 反对 -6, 当前有60, and the same five lines with the same values (it lists those for first, those against after; the framework keeps the game's order). Not compared on screen: any other action's acceptance.
- **`diplomacy.options ORL`** (an appanage) listed among its actions the scripted `promote_appanage_heir` (指定王储) and `annex` with the game's three reasons (ten years, +190 opinion, a seizure of land first).
- **Sent and answered**: `propose_alliance CAS` accepted, and Castile was an ally; `arrange_royal_marriage POR` and later `CAS` declined; `insult ENG` done; `send_gift SAV --amount 10` done, the treasury 66.44 → 56.44; `improve_relations ARA` done, after which `options ARA` listed `stop_improving_relations`, and that was done too; `embargo ENG` done; `ask_military_access NAV` accepted; `offer_loan ORL --amount 10` declined; `sell_province SAV --province 200 --price 100` declined; `offer_condottieri CAS --army 545460846646 --price 5` declined. The command log showed what each window would have written: `money=10.000`; `amount=10 duration=12 interest=1.000`; `province=200 amount=100`; `cost=5.000 unit={ id=127 type=54 }`.
- **Refused with the game's reasons**: a second diplomat to the same country ("最近已经派遣过外交官，所以直到1444年12月12日我们才能再次派遣。"), none left ("你没有外交官可供派遣。"), `send_gift --amount 100000` ("我们缺乏所需的金钱", after the diplomat's), `warn CAS` (too rich and strong), `declare_war CAS` on an ally (three reasons), `claim_throne CAS` (three), `transfer_trade_power ORL` (a subject), `sell_province SAV --province 183` ("巴黎距离萨伏依太远，无法将其核心化。"), `fabricate_claim SAV --province 200` (the network's size, and "蒙彼利埃已有核心。": the province given reached the game's check), `break_alliance SAV --ally CAS` (prestige).
- **Subjects**: the first `subject.options ORL` ended the game: the framework stopped it, "the framework used 0x1fd5238, which no dependency point checks" (the interactions' objects were not yet points of their own; they are now, each with its class). Then `subject.options ORL` listed 16 interactions with their names (安抚当地统治者, 免服兵役税, 转移贸易, 授予省份, …) and reasons (夺取宫廷资源: "属国的独立倾向必须低于25%。"). `subject.interact ORL scutage` was carried out, and `options` then showed it `active`; `placate_rulers` was carried out, and a second refused ("必须有正威望。", the full-width bullet then left in the reason, now taken off); `seize_court_resources` refused with its reason; an unknown key `invalid_argument`; Castile "not a subject of the player's".
- **Rivals**: `add_rival ENG`, `NAV` and `BUR` were carried out, and `country.get` listed them as rivals; a second `add_rival ENG` was refused with no reason; `remove_rival ENG` refused with "最近刚将英格兰设为宿敌，1449年11月11日前不能将其移除。", `remove_rival CAS` (no rival) with "需要花费100dip才能移除宿敌。". Before the game's own check was asked first, `add_rival NAV` had been carried out once and Navarre was no rival 35 days later; not looked into.
- **Not tried in the game**: most of the panel's other actions (each goes the same way as those sent: the panel's own action, its checks, its command); the acts whose window has the player choose a province, a country, an army or an amount beyond those above (`fabricate_claim` and `claim_states` carried out, `subsidize_war`, `transfer_trade_power`, `threaten_war`, `break_alliance`, the favors, `enforce_peace`, `intervene_in_war`, `demand_unlawful_territory`, `charter_company`, `appoint_cardinal`); those sent as the panel made them though their window chooses something the framework does not set (`support_rebels`, `share_maps`, `steal_maps`, `sell_ships`, `invite_to_federation`'s name); a scripted action carried out; annexing and integrating (no subject could be); the subject interactions that need a third country, an index or a name; Lua extensions calling any of it.
- **Cleanup**: the release put back every file the run changed and the game folder's framework files; `dev.toml` and the screenshots were deleted.

- **Startup** passed, every dependency point of the nine parts together.
- **A read of every group** answered: `country.get` (technology, ideas, governing capacity 198.54 of 400, inflation, loans, rebels), `province.get 183` (autonomy, culture, center of trade, cores, fort), `trade.nodes`, `technology.options` ("没有足够的行政点数研究这一级科技！……"), `focus.options`, `stability.options`, `economy.options`, `loan.list`, `building.options 183`, `province.options 183`, `leader.options`, `leader.list`, `mercenary.list`, `fort.list`, `diplomacy.options ENG`, `idea.list`, `policy.list` (empty), `government.options`, `government.reforms`, `estate.list`, `estate.interactions`, `parliament.options` (France has none), `decision.list`, `mission.list`, `missionary.options 183`, `missionary.automatic`, `colonist.options 183`, `trade_company.options 183`, `center_of_trade.options 183`, `merchant.list`; `peace.terms ENG` was refused, not at war.
- **Actions**: `province.develop_tax 183` took 65 adm, `economy.take_loan` a loan that `loan.list` then listed, `leader.recruit admiral` an admiral at 50 dip, `diplomacy.improve_relations ENG` answered `done`, `missionary.raise_automatic`, `estate.grant_privilege estate_church estate_church_land_rights` raised the governing capacity to 500; `building.build 183 marketplace` was refused "集市需要外交科技达到4级。".

## 2026-11-05 Scenarios (tested by me)

SC1 to SC3 of [scenarios.md](scenarios.md), with `cargo xtask scenario` from this branch's checkout, each run within a claim of its own that the runner made, waited for in the queue with `--wait`, and released. The game hidden throughout; the player's own install in the game folder before and after.

- **The list**: `cargo xtask scenario` with no name listed `perf`, `growth` and `leak`, each with its build, its minutes and its parameters with their values.
- **Busy**: with another checkout holding the game, `cargo xtask scenario perf` without `--wait` said the game was busy and that `--wait` waits in the queue, exited with 1 and made no report folder.
- **`perf`**: passed, on the player build, in 4.7 min: 47.2 s to the main menu, `menu.start MNG` 9.7 s, 0.81 cores paused and 1.75 at speed 5, 1444.11.11 to 1447.7.14 in 120.1 s, 487 game days a minute. The first try started the game twice, once for the main menu and once for the scenario's restart; the runner since brings an already running game to the main menu and leaves a game that is not running to the scenario's first step. Two later `perf` runs after another scenario passed too, with 305.6 and 453.3 game days a minute: the figure varies from run to run by more than the framework's setups are likely to differ.
- **`leak`**, two rounds with `watch 2`, `watch_after 1` and `quarantine true`: passed, on the heap census build, in 3.9 min. The report had every section `leak-run` wrote: the rounds, where their growth goes and each heap, the leftovers, what holds them, who let them go, what keeps the free pages (184 MB of pages no block is on), and the quarantine's (nothing written after a repair let it go).
- **Two builds in one run**: `growth perf --growth.rounds 2` built and installed the memory account build, played `growth`, then built and installed the player build and played `perf`, and released once.
- **The game ended from outside**: `eu4.exe` ended with `taskkill /F` while `growth` played.
  - During its first round's `menu.return`: `growth` came out failed, "the pipe broke", and `perf` after it passed. A return with "return without restarting" off ends its process on purpose, as the game starts again, so the scenario since takes a broken pipe in a return, with a game still running, for the return.
  - During the second round's first `menu.load`, twice. The first time `growth` came out failed too: the killed game stayed in the process list longer than the 15 s the runner gave it, so the runner did not see it end. Since then any other step whose pipe breaks counts as the game ending. The second time `growth` came out crashed, "the game ended while answering menu.load", with the round it had played. The report's crash section said that no crash folder came of it, as none does of a kill, and the release said the game did not crash.
- **`--from-report`** wrote `leak`'s `report.md` again from its `report.json`, the same byte for byte.
- **Every release** put every player file back ("every file is as the backup has it") and the game folder's framework files ("as they were").
- **Not tried**:
  - a step taking longer than its time, so hung is not verified: steps have 600 s, and no scenario has a parameter to shorten one;
  - the player taking the game while a scenario plays, since a session never claims as the player, so unfinished is not verified;
  - a real crash with a dump, so the crash section's reading of a dump (the exception and the stack) is not verified;
  - a save the scenario names missing;
  - a run within a claim this checkout already held;
  - the lease renewed by the runner after 20 minutes, since no run here lasted that long; a renewal at each start was seen;
  - `leak` and `growth` with their 10 rounds.
- **Cleanup**: the runs' report folders and logs were deleted after the figures above were taken from them; the queue held only other checkouts' claims.

## 2026-11-05 The player language and fonts by language (tested by me)

L2 and L3 of [player-language.md](player-language.md), the player build of this branch, with the player's playset (a Chinese language mod, the game in English) in a borderless 3840x2160 window, hidden, on a Windows that displays Simplified Chinese. The framework settings named no language and chose the three FZ fonts for body text, headings and the map, as the player had.

- **The first start**: the settings got `language="simp_chinese"`, the log said "the language is simp_chinese, as Windows displays it", and the report "language: simp_chinese (chosen in the framework settings); the game runs in english". The three FZ fonts were kept and drew; the console, never chosen, drew in Consolas and was not written into the file. The old `text_appearance` line was dropped, as before.
- **The settings window**: 语言 简体中文 at the top; its list showed the twelve by what each calls itself. In FZPingXianYaSongS-R-GB, Русский, the ê of Português and the ü of Türkçe came out wide apart: the font has them as full-width GB2312 glyphs. In Segoe UI they were drawn as they should be.
- **A change**: English chosen and saved wrote `language="english"` and kept the three chosen fonts. With their lines then taken out of the file, the next start drew body text in Segoe UI 400, headings in Segoe UI 600, the map in Georgia and the console in Consolas, and the settings window was in English with Segoe UI, its heading weight Semibold.
- **Simplified Chinese's defaults**: the file set to `simp_chinese` with no fonts, the next start drew body text in FZPingXianYaSongS-R-GB, headings in FZYaSongS-B-GB and the map in FZShengShiKaiShuS-EB-GB.
- **Not tried**: the settings window with its pages, which master gave it after this run: the language on its first page, 语言与字体; a Windows that displays another language, or none of the twelve; the other ten languages' defaults (Traditional Chinese, Japanese and Korean have their fonts on this machine, untested); a mod's file in a language not on the list; a font chosen in the settings window and saved; `eu4-harmony new` with a language from Windows (unit tests only).
- **Cleanup**: releasing the game put back the player's files, the framework's `settings.txt` with its `text_appearance` line among them, and the game folder's framework files; the screenshots were deleted.

## 2026-11-05 The bugs reviewed: reproduced, then fixed (tested by me)

A review of the code found bugs across the framework; those simple to show in the game were tried on master (8735166) first, then on the branch with every fix. Both runs within a claim, started hidden, new games as France at 1444.11.11 driven through `eu4-harmony game …`, the player's playset; the event of the Hundred Years' War answered first each time.

- **Before, on master**:
  - `time advance 365 --speed 1`, then `menu return` 4 s later: the return answered `main_menu`, and the advance never answered until its client gave up at 90 s.
  - After that return, `session get` sent 2 s into a `menu start FRA`: `session_changed`, "it was sent in session 3, which ended before it ran; this is session 4".
  - `menu start FRA` from within a game: `session_ended` with `reason` `return`.
  - `peace terms REB`: `internal`, "invalid read 0x0+0".
  - After the console's `favors PRO 50`, `trade_favors_for_trust PRO --favors 10 --trust 5`, the window's own step ("花费10人情来交换5信任度。"), was accepted, and `options PRO` then refused it with "你已经有拥有普罗旺斯的完全信任。": full trust from one step.
  - `sell_province BUR --province 183`, Paris, the capital: refused only with "巴黎距离勃艮第太远，无法将其核心化。". `--province 87`, England's Calais: "我们并不是加莱的所有者", so the game's check does look at the owner.
  - `sell_ships PRO`, sent with no ships and a price of -1 as the panel makes it: not refused by the game's check; Provence `declined`. The game went on.
- **After, on the branch**:
  - The advance answered as the return's did, `stopped` `main_menu`, "the game returned to the main menu".
  - The `session get` sent during the start waited and answered session 2.
  - `menu start FRA` from a game: `reason` `new_game`.
  - `peace terms REB`: `rejected`, "the player is not at war with REB".
  - Favors for trust: 15 favors refused (the window spends 10 at a time), `--trust 5000` refused (10 favors buy 5), 60 favors refused (Provence owed 50, its trust 50); 10 favors with no trust given accepted, and `options` then refused it only for the diplomat, not for full trust.
  - The panel's limits: Paris refused as the capital, Calais as not the player's, `sell_ships` `unavailable`, a gift of 0, a loan of 100000 ducats with 71 in the treasury and war subsidies for 5 refused `invalid_argument`; a gift of 10 `done`.
  - The console's `tag CAS`: "Now Playing - 卡斯蒂利亚", then `session_ended` `reason` `country` and session 4.
  - The framework settings with a byte order mark before `language="english"` (Windows displays Simplified Chinese): the report said "language: english (chosen in the framework settings)", and the file was not written (its MD5 the same before and after).
- **Found in the run**: the claim's first release put every file back and said the game was free, but its record stayed, held, with the watch keeping it: two checkouts waiting in the queue on master's xtask had written the record again without the mark that says who puts it back. Fixed on the branch and released again with it; the queue went on about 5 minutes late.
- **Not tried in the game**: an ironman exit save that fails; `console.run` with a command that loads a save (it needs the console's developer lock off); an input dropped for another frame size; the Lua time limit and memory limit (unit tests only); `fabricate_claim`, `claim_states`, `trade_favors_for_core`, `transfer_trade_power`, `offer_condottieri` with the new checks (no spy network, no favors or no core to try them on); the framework UI's glyph page filling and a touchpad's wheel; the claim's new marks, beyond one claim, its watch and its release; a scenario run stopped by Ctrl-C or an error.
- **Cleanup**: each release put the player's files back, every file as its backup had it, and the game folder's framework files.

## 2026-11-05 The music volume the mixer passes to SDL (tested by me)

A player found music at a low volume setting coming and going, sometimes silent. This run measured what the game's mixer (`0x16a6c20`) hands SDL for the music. A developer build with the call trace on `SDL_MixAudioFormat`'s body, `0x17458b0`; the game's `0x1735360` and the dynamic API's default `0x172e8a0` only jump to it through SDL's jump table at `0x1fb0000`, slot `+0x1b0`, which SDL fills at its start. Hidden starts, without sound: SDL's `dummy` driver still runs the mixer. Main menu only, no save loaded; `master_volume` and `music_volume` set in the player's `settings.txt` before each start.

| `master_volume` | `music_volume` | Loading screen | Main menu |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 12 | 67.32 (the player's) | 10 | 8 |
| 100 | 100 | 128 | 96 |
| 100 | 10 | 13 | 10 |
| 12 | 3 | 1 | 1 |

- **The level** is the gain times 128, rounded, out of SDL's 128: the gain is master × music × the sound's own, all linear in amplitude, so a level is that fraction of the sample's amplitude. At the main menu a further 0.75 applies; whether it is the main theme's own volume or the menu's was not looked into.
- **Never 0 above 0**: master 12 with music 3 gives 0.46, which rounds to 0, yet SDL was given 1. A gain above zero plays at level 1 at least, about -42 dB.
- **The player's setting**: master 12 leaves the whole music slider levels 0 to 15; the player's music plays at level 8 to 10 out of 128, about -22 dB.
- **The tracks**: measured offline with ffmpeg's `ebur128`, the vanilla tracks' integrated loudness runs from -9.9 LUFS (`event_war_battleofbreitenfeld`) to -24.3 (`kingscourt`), and within a track the loudness range is up to 18.9 LU (`kingsinthenorth`, `maintheme`). No vanilla `music.asset` entry sets a `volume`; in the DLCs' music `.asset` files, 42 entries set one, from 0.35 to 0.9. A quiet track at level 8, against sound effects and ambient sound at 77 and 76, is the coming and going the player heard.
- **Not verified**: music in a running game and its war and peace tracks; fades between tracks, none of which these runs crossed; what was heard, since no sound was played; the 0.75 at the main menu.
- **Cleanup**: releasing the game put back the player's files, `settings.txt` with its volumes among them, and the game folder's framework files; `dev.toml` was deleted, and the copied logs were in the session's scratch folder.

## 2026-11-05 Player actions follow-ups: subjects, government, and what A2, A3 and A5 only saw refused (tested by me)

Developer builds of this branch with the command log (`subject`, `diplomaticactioncommand`, the parliament and mission commands), within one claim, started hidden; the player's playset, 3840x2160. Three processes: the player's save `阿尔达比勒1462_08_30` loaded (its four mods in the playset) and played as Muscovy, then Hungary, then Muscovy again through the console's `tag`; and two new games as England at 1444.11.11. The console's `cash`, `adm`, `dip`, `mil`, `winwars` and `run` (a file of effects in the user folder, deleted after: `add_trust`, `add_prestige`, `add_base_tax`, `add_spy_network_in`) set the cases up; the game's questions were answered with their first option.

- **Annexing and integrating** (A6): Muscovy's AI was annexing Beloozero; `diplomacy.cancel_annexation BLO` was done and the panel then offered `annex` with the game's reasons (no diplomat, "+190" opinion). Once a diplomat was back, `annex PSK` was done, and `options` gave `cancel_annexation` with "吞并进度0.0%（0/162），预计将在1469年7月完成。" and its monthly 2 dip; the outliner showed "吞并/合并 0.0% 正在吞并". Hungary's AI was integrating Croatia (104/294); `cancel_integration CRO` was done, `integrate` refused for want of a diplomat, then done after twelve days, its progress started again at 0/294 (the game's rule), the outliner's diplomat "0.0% 合并".
- **Subject interactions with a province** (A6): the interactions' methods read the command's `index` as a province (research/subjects.md), and `subject.interact` took a `province` for it. `grant_province CRO --province 1864` gave Croatia the province with its core (the game's report said so), one not Hungary's was refused with no reason; `seize_territory PRM --province 1077` made it Muscovy's, Perm's core kept; `grant_core_claim PRM --province 305` changed the claim on Kazan's province from Muscovy's to Perm's, one of Muscovy's own refused with no reason.
- **The panel's acts with a window's choice** (A6, one of each kind the diplomats allowed): `subsidize_war KAZ --amount 1 --duration 20` done (`warsubsidy={ … duration=20 amount=1.000 }`, then `stop_war_subsidies` offered); `transfer_trade_power`, `threaten_war`, `break_alliance`, `share_maps`, `designate_march`, `steer_trade` refused with "最近已经派遣过外交官…" and "你没有外交官可供派遣。"; `fabricate_claim LIT --province 269` refused with "没有可伪造宣称的接壤省份。". **Found**: `support_rebels LIT`, sent with no faction as the panel made it, answered `done` and supported nothing (the panel still offered it). It now takes the faction's kind: as England with a spy network in Scotland, `support_rebels SCO --rebels anti_tax_rebels` posted `rebel={ id=117 type=50 }` and the panel then said "我们在苏格兰境内支持的叛军当前的叛乱几率为0%。我们的支持将持续到1449年11月。"; a kind Scotland had not was refused by the framework. `share_maps CAS` with no region was `accepted`; what it shared was not looked at.
- **Buildings and states** (A3): `building.build 295 temple` took 100.3 ducats and the outliner showed "建筑 233d 教堂" (to 1463.6.19, as `building.options` said); `building.cancel 295` gave the 100.3 back. `province.make_territory 1079` made Galich a territory at 50 autonomy; Moscow, the capital's state, was refused with "不能取消首都所在的直属州。". The make-state and make-territory reasons carry the button's own line after them ("（点击设置为自治领地）"), as A3 decided.
- **The treasury and technology** (A2): bank loans taken one by one were refused at the 56th, "你无法从银行获得更多贷款。" and "- 你已经借贷了56笔贷款，无法获得超过56笔贷款！"; the second kept the check's `- `, which is now left out. Mercantilism raised 90 times reached 100 and was refused with "我们已经拥有了最高的重商主义倾向". Each technology researched to 32 (points refilled from the console) was then refused "the adm technology is at its last level", while its `technology.options` entry had no reason; it now gives the same.
- **War and peace** (A5): war declared on Kazan with no ally called; Circassia's call to arms read `accepts` false, total -5; after `add_trust` +30 it read true, 25, and `call_to_arms CIR` was `accepted`, Circassia among the attackers. After `winwars`, `peace.evaluate CHG []` read East Chagatai accepting ("被彻底击败" +100), and `peace.demand CHG [gold 20]` was `accepted`: East Chagatai and its three subjects left the war, Kazan and Crimea stayed.
- **Government** (A7, England): `parliament.options` offered ten issues; `debate fund_expansion_of_bureaucracy` began it, every other issue was then refused "a debate is going on", and the parliament view (the first of the three buttons at the lower right) showed "辩论议案：拨款扩大公职体系" and six seats, Kent (234) among them after `add_seat 234`; London, already seated, and Paris were refused with no reason. `remove_seat` was refused by the game's check for London and Kent, during the debate and in a new game without one; the check (`0x2a4110`) asks the parliament, a modifier `0x249`, the seat, `0x9623a0` (a DLC check and the parliament's `+0x61`) and `0xacccf0`, and which failed was not found. `grant_consessions_to_the_church`'s name read "向让步": its text names `$ESTATE_CHURCH$`, which the lookup left out; names now fill such a key in (not seen again, the issue not offered in the second game). After `add_prestige` and 700 base tax in London (the total counted at the month), `raise_rank` made England an empire (rank 3) and the rank was then refused "你的政府等级已达最高！"; the rank's earlier reasons started with "－", the check's `- ` then the text's own dash, which are now both left out. `eng_recruit_the_mercenaries` read only "陆军数量至少为上限的80.0%" once the three hired companies arrived (`mercenary.hire` takes days: the outliner showed "征募部队 6d"), then `can`; `mission.complete` completed it and a second was refused "the mission is completed". The estates view showed the clergy 50% 22%, nobles 50% 34%, burghers 50% 27%, land 25%, 19%, 25%: `estate.list` gave 22.613, 34.772, 27.614 and land 25.227, 19.545, 25.228, the view rounding down. No reform was offered free (every change cost 50 or more); the framework posts every change paying.
- **Not reached**: changing a reform for free; taking a seat away; `upgrade_subject_type` and `start_colonial_war`; a technology's or a loan's reasons after the fixes, and an issue's name filled in, in the game (unit tests only); an extension calling any of it.
